- `penrosetiling`：潘洛斯拼貼——深藍黑底上淺藍色細線畫出一塊潘洛斯菱形非週期拼貼補丁（德布魯因五重網格構造；149個菱形：95胖、54瘦；323條邊、175個頂點、歐拉示性數1），seed把補丁旋轉36°的整數倍；測試以獨立重寫的五重網格程式確認結構並逐seed比對；只是一小塊補丁、不是無限拼貼；作者示意，未引用特定來源也沒有對照照片。0.5 m tile／0.0007 UV relief（非公尺深度），metallic0；每個取樣點走訪323條邊，未量測耗時。目前目錄991種，含plain的gallery992項。

- `kagome`：籠目——深橄欖綠底上淺稻草色細線的籠目格子（連接蜂巢每個六邊形六條邊的中點，形成三向直線的六邊形與三角形格；7欄×8列、336條線段），seed以1/64磚平移；與tulle、kikko共用蜂巢線段sampler分支（新增`outer`與`innerDegrees`欄位，兩者行為不變）；四個seed的取樣與測試中獨立重寫的模型一致；作者示意，未引用特定來源也沒有對照照片，不含上下交織。0.3 m tile／0.0009 UV relief（非公尺深度），metallic0；每個取樣點走訪336條線段，未量測耗時。目前目錄990種，含plain的gallery991項。

- `kikko`：龜甲紋——深棕底上琥珀色細線的龜甲形六角蜂巢（每個六邊形內有半徑一半的同心小六邊形；7欄×8列、56個六邊形、504條線段），seed以1/64磚平移；與tulle共用蜂巢線段sampler分支（新增`inner`欄位，tulle為0、行為不變）；四個seed的取樣與測試中獨立重寫的模型一致；作者示意，未引用特定來源也沒有對照照片，只有同心六邊形線。0.3 m tile／0.0009 UV relief（非公尺深度），metallic0；每個取樣點走訪504條線段，未量測耗時。目前目錄989種，含plain的gallery990項。

- `vermiculation`：蟲跡雕紋——淺棕褐色石面上刻有彎曲分叉、互不相連的深色蠕蟲狀溝槽（約20到30條、占約35%面積），圖案來自`feed 0.078`、`kill 0.061`的週期性Gray-Scott反應擴散，seed改變初始擾動；與turingmaze共用sampler分支（新增`baseHeight`欄位，turingmaze為0、行為不變）；作者示意，未引用特定來源也沒有對照照片。0.4 m tile／0.0015 UV relief（非公尺深度），metallic0。目前目錄988種，含plain的gallery989項。

- `minkowskisausage`：閔考斯基香腸——深藍黑底上淡藍色細線描出第2階二次科赫島（256步、256個相異格點的簡單封閉迴圈、768個亮node，線寬三分之一格），seed沿環面平移；與dragoncurve、peanocurve、levycurve共用走線sampler分支；與測試中以區塊遞迴獨立寫出的曲線逐node一致；作者示意，未引用特定來源也沒有對照照片。0.25 m tile／0.0008 UV relief（非公尺深度），metallic0。目前目錄987種，含plain的gallery988項。

- `pisanotable`：皮薩諾週期表——48×48個圓角方塊磚，第n格（1到2304）依費波那契數列模n的皮薩諾週期與上界6n的比值決定色調（只有n = 10、50、250、1250達到上界、最亮），seed沿環面平移整張表；磚面與測試中獨立以數對產生法寫出的週期表逐磚一致；作者示意，未引用特定來源也沒有對照照片，預覽上近似雜訊。0.3 m tile／0.0008 UV relief（非公尺深度），metallic0；建構時間未量測。目前目錄986種，含plain的gallery987項。

- `mobiustable`：莫比烏斯表——48×48個圓角方塊磚，第n格（1到2304）依μ(n)分三類：μ = 0（有平方因子，904個）暗、μ = +1（699個）亮紫、μ = −1（701個）灰紫，4的倍數形成暗直條，seed沿環面平移整張表；磚面與測試中以完整質因數分解獨立算出的表逐磚一致；作者示意，未引用特定來源也沒有對照照片。0.3 m tile／0.0008 UV relief（非公尺深度），metallic0。目前目錄985種，含plain的gallery986項。

- `abundantnumbers`：盈數表——48×48個圓角方塊磚，第n格（1到2304）依σ(n)與2n的比較分三類：虧數（1,732個）暗、盈數（569個）較亮、完全數（6、28、496）最亮，12與6的倍數形成亮直條，seed沿環面平移整張表；磚面與測試中以試除法獨立算出的分類表逐磚一致；作者示意，未引用特定來源也沒有對照照片。0.3 m tile／0.0008 UV relief（非公尺深度），metallic0。目前目錄984種，含plain的gallery985項。

- `xortable`：互斥或表——64×64個圓角方塊磚，第(row, col)格的色調正比於row XOR col（主對角線最暗、越大越亮的萊姆綠，呈巢狀謝爾賓斯基式方塊，即XOR texture），seed沿環面平移；磚面與測試中獨立寫出的表逐磚一致；作者示意，未引用特定來源也沒有對照照片。0.3 m tile／0.0008 UV relief（非公尺深度），metallic0。目前目錄983種，含plain的gallery984項。

- `paleytable`：佩利表——61×61個圓角方塊磚，第(row, col)格是col − row對質數61的勒壤得符號（對角線中間色調、平方剩餘亮玫瑰粉、非剩餘暗紫紅），表只依賴col − row所以呈斜向帶狀，seed沿環面平移；磚面與測試中獨立寫出的表逐磚一致；作者示意，未引用特定來源也沒有對照照片。0.3 m tile／0.0008 UV relief（非公尺深度），metallic0。目前目錄982種，含plain的gallery983項。

- `levycurve`：列維曲線——深青黑底上薄荷綠細線描出第9階列維C曲線（512步、416個相異格點、462條相異邊、1,340個亮node，線寬三分之一格），seed沿環面平移；與dragoncurve、peanocurve共用走線sampler分支；與測試中獨立以位元計數寫出的曲線逐node一致；作者示意，未引用特定來源也沒有對照照片，曲線不封閉。0.25 m tile／0.0008 UV relief（非公尺深度），metallic0。目前目錄980種，含plain的gallery981項。

- `mandelbrottiles`：曼德博集合磚——64×64個圓角方塊磚的曼德博集合鑲嵌，色調為各格中心的逃逸迭代次數（最多48次，開平方）、永不逃逸者（726格）最暗，seed沿環面平移整張表；盤面與測試中獨立重寫的逃逸時間逐磚一致；作者示意，未引用特定來源也沒有對照照片，像素風、環面接縫不連續。0.3 m tile／0.0008 UV relief（非公尺深度），metallic0。目前目錄973種，含plain的gallery974項。

- `sherpafleece`：雪帕刷毛布——大量任意旋轉、邊緣不規則的奶油色團塊疊成蓬鬆捲簇，偶有受遮蔽的淺褐縫；重用`granola`的兩層橢圓塊sampler；作者示意，未引用特定來源也沒有對照照片，預覽更像奶油色碎屑／爆米花而不像真實纖維。0.15 m tile／0.012 UV relief（非公尺深度），metallic0。目前目錄944種，含plain的gallery945項。

- `tilledsoil`：翻耕土壤——深褐縫隙地上疊著任意旋轉、邊緣不規則的中褐色土塊與少量淺色卵石；重用`granola`的兩層橢圓塊sampler；作者示意，未引用特定來源也沒有對照照片，預覽也像迷彩，比真實土壤平坦。0.4 m tile／0.012 UV relief（非公尺深度），metallic0。目前目錄943種，含plain的gallery944項。

- `heatsinkfins`：散熱鰭片——陽極處理擠型鋁散熱片，每tile 20片直條鰭片、平坦鰭頂與深而受遮蔽的窄縫、鰭頂的細擠型線與淡斑駁；整面金屬（metallic 1）；作者示意，未引用特定來源也沒有對照照片，無鰭片高度、基板或熱管。0.1 m tile／0.01 UV relief（非公尺深度）。目前目錄942種，含plain的gallery943項。

- `jerky`：牛肉乾——深紅褐色底上沿u方向的細密肌纖維、寬低頻色調變化、光澤變化與零星近黑胡椒碎粒；作者示意，未引用特定來源也沒有對照照片，預覽也像髮絲拋光的暗色木紋，缺乏真實肉乾的形狀。0.12 m tile／0.004 UV relief（非公尺深度），metallic0。目前目錄941種，含plain的gallery942項。

- `garlicskin`：蒜皮——奶油白紙質底上約11條沿v方向、被雜訊扭曲且寬窄變化的淡紫色縱向條紋，帶縱向纖維、柔和皺褶與細grain；作者示意，未引用特定來源也沒有對照照片，預覽較像條紋紙而不太像真實蒜皮。0.1 m tile／0.003 UV relief（非公尺深度），metallic0。目前目錄940種，含plain的gallery941項。

- `pancake`：煎餅——金黃底上帶蕾絲邊的低頻焦褐斑塊（面積隨seed差異大）與散布的微小氣孔凹洞；作者示意，未引用特定來源也沒有對照照片，斑塊邊緣有方塊雜訊感，無光澤與厚度。0.25 m tile／0.004 UV relief（非公尺深度），metallic0。目前目錄939種，含plain的gallery940項。

- `granola`：燕麥穀物脆片——蜂蜜褐色底面上疊著兩層任意角度、邊緣不規則的橢圓塊（較大的淡奶油色燕麥片與較小的深色堅果／果乾塊），底面外露處受遮蔽變暗；作者示意，未引用特定來源也沒有對照照片，預覽也像迷彩，比真實脆片平坦。0.2 m tile／0.008 UV relief（非公尺深度），metallic0。目前目錄937種，含plain的gallery938項。

- `raspberry`：覆盆子——16×18排成交錯格、略為重疊的圓形紅色小核果（各有色調、穹頂明暗與高光），顆粒間為較暗、受遮蔽的細縫；作者示意，未引用特定來源也沒有對照照片，比真實覆盆子規則許多，無絨毛、花萼或整體形狀。0.08 m tile／0.006 UV relief（非公尺深度），metallic0。目前目錄936種，含plain的gallery937項。

- `salami`：薩拉米——帶低頻斑駁的深紅色醃肉切面，散布大小不一、相鄰者相連的乳白橢圓脂肪斑與細小近黑胡椒粒；作者示意，未引用特定來源也沒有對照照片，比真實香腸規則許多，無外皮、黴層或肌纖維。0.12 m tile／0.002 UV relief（非公尺深度），metallic0。目前目錄935種，含plain的gallery936項。

- `baconstrips`：培根片——約5條被低頻雜訊扭成波浪的紅色瘦肉帶與乳白色肥肉帶交錯（瘦肉有沿u方向的纖維，肥肉較亮、略凸起且較光滑，交界略暗）；作者示意，未引用特定來源也沒有對照照片，比真實培根平滑規則，無皺縮、油脂氣泡或肉皮。0.2 m tile／0.003 UV relief（非公尺深度），metallic0。目前目錄934種，含plain的gallery935項。

- `jarrah`：賈拉木（Eucalyptus marginata）縱向木面，磚紅偏棕底、中到粗質地、2條很淡的暗帶、縱向纖維與散布細暗導管短痕；無淡色邊材、膠線、捲曲／交錯紋或塗層。依實際閱讀[Wood Database Jarrah](https://www.wood-database.com/jarrah/)與實看[木面照片](https://www.wood-database.com/wp-content/uploads/jarrah-s-400x400.jpg)近似，僅作配色與紋理形態參考。重用既有導管sampler，0.18 m tile／0.0025 UV relief（非公尺深度），metallic0。目前目錄926種，含plain的gallery927項。

- `amberbubbles`：含氣泡的琥珀樹脂——橘黃色光滑樹脂，3條被整數波數正弦彎曲的流動明暗帶；14×14格中約45%有被困住的氣泡（隨機大小位置，略凸、更光滑、帶亮緣與光暈），另有低頻混濁；作者示意，未引用特定來源，無半透明、折射、內含物或裂紋。0.12 m tile／0.004 UV relief（非公尺深度），metallic0。目前目錄925種，含plain的gallery926項。

- `tapemeasure`：捲尺——一個tile為黃色捲尺的100 mm段：100條毫米刻線、每5 mm較長、每10 mm最長而較粗，近黑刻線印在黃色塗層上，橫截面為兩緣升起中央下凹的餘弦弧；作者示意，未引用特定來源，無數字、英制刻度、端鉤、外殼或磨損。0.1 m tile／0.03 UV relief（非公尺深度），metallic0。目前目錄912種，含plain的gallery913項。

- `ledmatrix`：LED點陣面板——48×48顆圓形LED、8×6個5×7字元格，每格隨seed點亮2–4條隨機偽筆畫（不是字型）；亮LED紅橘高亮、暗LED暗紅，透鏡光滑略凸，黑色底板較粗糙帶AO；作者示意，未引用特定來源，無emission、捲動、混色或RGB子像素。0.16 m tile／0.004 UV relief（非公尺深度），metallic0。目前目錄911種，含plain的gallery912項。

- `barcodelabel`：條碼標籤——一個tile是一張標籤寬度（左右各10模組靜區、Code 128 Start B 樣式、9個隨seed抽出的11模組資料符號、13模組停止樣式），近黑碳粉條在白色粗糙紙面上、上下留白；**不可解碼**（無檢查碼、無奇偶限制、無人眼可讀文字）。結構依[Wikipedia「Code 128」](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Code_128)，Start／停止位元樣式未由該頁驗證。0.14 m tile／0.0006 UV relief（非公尺深度），metallic0。目前目錄910種，含plain的gallery911項。

- `topographicmap`：等高線地形圖紙——暖米白紙面上以定值寬度的棕色等高線印出由7個整數波數正弦疊成的地形（16階、每5條一條較粗較深的計曲線）、極淡高度色調與紙纖維grain；作者示意，未引用特定來源，無真實地形、標高註記、水系、道路或圖例。0.3 m tile／0.0015 UV relief（非公尺深度），metallic0。目前目錄909種，含plain的gallery910項。

- `eastindianrosewood`：東印度黃檀（Dalbergia latifolia）縱向木面，深紫褐底、5條較暗近黑的起伏條紋、縱向纖維與散布短小開放導管凹點；無金褐淺色面、淺色邊材、拋光或交錯紋。依實際閱讀[Wood Database East Indian Rosewood](https://www.wood-database.com/east-indian-rosewood/)與實看[木面照片](https://www.wood-database.com/wp-content/uploads/dalbergia-latifolia-1-300x600.jpg)近似，僅作配色與紋理形態參考。重用既有導管sampler，0.18 m tile／0.0025 UV relief（非公尺深度），metallic0。目前目錄908種，含plain的gallery909項。

- `lenticularsheet`：柱狀透鏡印刷片——每tile 32條平行圓柱透鏡，頂亮而光滑、縫低且較粗糙並帶AO，藍銀底色由斜向翻轉條紋與8×8低頻影像疊成；作者示意，未引用特定來源，無真實多幀交錯影像、視角動畫或折射。0.1 m tile／0.008 UV relief（非公尺深度），metallic0。目前目錄907種，含plain的gallery908項。

- `europeanlarch`：歐洲落葉松（Larix decidua）弦切面，淡粉紅褐／淡黃褐早材底、12條流動拱形的紅褐晚材年輪線（約占面積四分之一）與細縱向纖維；無樹脂囊、節、邊／心材界線、螺旋紋或塗層。依實際閱讀[Wood Database European Larch](https://www.wood-database.com/european-larch/)與實看[弦切面照片](https://www.wood-database.com/wp-content/uploads/european-larch-s-400x400.jpg)近似，僅作配色與紋理形態參考。重用`douglasfir`年輪sampler，0.2 m tile／0.003 UV relief（非公尺深度），metallic0。目前目錄906種，含plain的gallery907項。

- `shagbarkhickory`：粗皮山胡桃木（Carya ovata）縱向木面，淡黃褐／淡棕底、中等質地、3條較暖淺褐起伏條紋、縱向纖維與散布短小開放導管凹點；無紅褐深色心材主導、環孔早材導管列、交錯／波浪紋或塗層。依實際閱讀[Wood Database Shagbark Hickory](https://www.wood-database.com/shagbark-hickory/)與實看[木面照片](https://www.wood-database.com/wp-content/uploads/shagbark-hickory-400x400.jpg)近似，僅作配色與紋理形態參考。重用既有導管sampler，0.18 m tile／0.002 UV relief（非公尺深度），metallic0。目前目錄905種，含plain的gallery906項。

- `monitorlizardskin`：巨蜥風格顆粒狀鱗皮——14欄×16列六角緊密排列的圓形隆起鱗片（奇數列錯半欄），每片各有色調、較亮較光滑，鱗間為窄深暗溝（AO 0.4），4條橫向深淺色帶，橄欖褐色；作者示意，未引用特定物種或來源，無腹鱗、脊或皮褶。0.14 m tile／0.01 UV relief（非公尺深度），metallic0。目前目錄904種，含plain的gallery905項。

- `sitkaspruce`：Sitka spruce（Picea sitchensis）縱向木面，奶白偏淡黃底、極細而幾乎筆直的極低對比縱向年輪線與細纖維grain；無bearclaw、粉紅色調、樹脂道、節疤或塗層。依實際閱讀[Wood Database Sitka Spruce](https://www.wood-database.com/sitka-spruce/)與實看[木面照片](https://www.wood-database.com/wp-content/uploads/sitka-spruce-400x400.jpg)近似，僅作配色與年輪線形態參考。重用`douglasfir`年輪sampler，0.12 m tile／0.002 UV relief（非公尺深度），metallic0。目前目錄903種，含plain的gallery904項。

- `honduranmahogany`：宏都拉斯桃花心木（Swietenia macrophylla）縱向木面，偏深紅褐色、中等均勻質地、7條寬而幾乎筆直的低對比緞帶狀條紋與極稀疏短小開放導管凹點；無新切淺粉褐、經年變深、交錯／波浪紋、巢狀年輪、chatoyancy或塗層。依實際閱讀[Wood Database Honduran Mahogany](https://www.wood-database.com/honduran-mahogany/)與實看[木面照片](https://www.wood-database.com/wp-content/uploads/swietenia-macrophylla-alt.jpg)近似，僅作配色與紋理形態參考。重用既有導管sampler，0.18 m tile／0.0015 UV relief（非公尺深度），metallic0。目前目錄902種，含plain的gallery903項。

- `woodenpalletdeck`：木棧板面板——一個tile內4塊沿u方向的木板，板間為低窄深縫（約9%寬、AO 0.2），每板有各自色調、起伏年輪線、縱向纖維與細grain，板中線每tile 2顆深色略凸圓釘頭；非完整棧板（無橫梁／板端／裂縫／節疤），釘頭為dielectric。概念依實際閱讀[Wikipedia「Pallet」](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pallet)導言，尺寸與色彩為作者設定。0.4 m tile／0.006 UV relief（非公尺深度），metallic0。目前目錄901種，含plain的gallery902項。

- `forgedcarbon`：鍛造碳纖維風格複合材——10×10格、每格5個隨機位置／方向／長寬／灰階與疊放權重的圓角長條碎片覆蓋近黑樹脂背景，碎片內有沿長軸的細密編織漣漪，整體亮面（roughness約0.18–0.30）；非規則斜紋／平紋（既有`carbonfiber`），無環氧厚度或金屬光澤。作者示意，未引用特定製程或來源。0.2 m tile／0.0012 UV relief（非公尺深度），metallic0。目前目錄900種，含plain的gallery901項。

- `popcornceiling`：爆米花／吸音天花板噴塗——20×20格各一個隨機偏移、半徑與高度不同的圓形顆粒，以`1−Π(1−顆粒)`軟合併成起伏顆粒團，顆粒間是被遮蔽的低谷（AO約0.55），偏白帶細斑點grain；無水漬、剝落、刮平區、纖維或接縫。概念依實際閱讀[Wikipedia「Popcorn ceiling」](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Popcorn_ceiling)導言，尺寸與色彩為作者設定，只描述外觀、不含也不暗示成分。0.25 m tile／0.006 UV relief（非公尺深度），metallic0。目前目錄899種，含plain的gallery900項。

- `kilimdiamonds`：kilim風格階梯菱形平織——64×64緯線格，每16格方塊的中心與四角各有四環同心階梯菱形（深褐／奶白／灰褐／奶白，角上菱形色序對調），每列微小色差、每格橫向緯線脊與細纖維grain。概念依實際閱讀[Wikipedia「Kilim」](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kilim)（平織tapestry地毯），圖案與色彩為作者設定、未引用特定紋樣；無流蘇、邊飾、起絨或斷緯。0.32 m tile／0.003 UV relief（非公尺深度），metallic0。目前目錄898種，含plain的gallery899項。

- `teak`：柚木（Tectona grandis）縱向木面，金黃到中棕底、直而不均勻的粗質地、長而起伏的深棕縱向條紋與散布的短小開放導管凹點；無老化深色、邊材、波浪／交錯figure、油亮塗層或導管內淺色沉積。依實際閱讀[Wood Database Teak](https://www.wood-database.com/teak/)與實看[木面照片](https://www.wood-database.com/wp-content/uploads/tectona-grandis.jpg)近似，僅作配色與紋理形態參考。重用既有導管sampler，0.2 m tile／0.003 UV relief（非公尺深度），metallic0。目前目錄897種，含plain的gallery898項。

- `blackcherry`：美洲黑櫻桃木（Prunus serotina）縱向木面，均勻中等紅褐色、細平直縱紋、極低對比寬弱帶與極稀疏短小深色導管凹點；無邊材條帶、捲紋、樹膠斑或塗層。依實際閱讀[Wood Database Black Cherry](https://www.wood-database.com/black-cherry/)與實看[木面照片](https://www.wood-database.com/wp-content/uploads/prunus-serotina.jpg)近似，僅作配色與紋理形態參考。重用既有導管sampler，0.18 m tile／0.0015 UV relief（非公尺深度），metallic0。目前目錄896種，含plain的gallery897項。

- `golfballdimples`：高爾夫球風格凹坑——7欄×8列六角緊密排列的圓形淺球碗（奇數列錯半欄），碗心較低、較暗（AO 0.55）、較粗糙，碗間為窄而光滑的平台，偏白亮面塑膠。作者平面示意，未引用特定球種或來源；非完整球體曲率、接縫或印字。0.028 m tile／0.012 UV relief（非公尺深度），metallic0。目前目錄895種，含plain的gallery896項。

- `laptopkeycaps`：chiclet筆電鍵帽——8×8格方陣，每格一顆圓角方形（四次方超橢圓）深色霧面鍵帽，鍵頂中央微凹、鍵緣有高光，鍵間為深色窄縫（AO 0.25）；每鍵色調略異，含霧面細grain。作者示意，未引用特定產品或來源，無字符、背光、鍵軸或機身。0.152 m tile／0.004 UV relief（非公尺深度），metallic0。目前目錄894種，含plain的gallery895項。

- `qrcodelabel`：QR風格印刷標籤——29×29模組加四模組白色安靜區填滿一個tile，三角7×7 finder pattern與separator、第6列／行timing、右下5×5 alignment、依seed的偽隨機資料模組，深色油墨印在帶纖維grain的淺紙上。**不是可解碼QR**（無訊息、格式資訊、遮罩或錯誤更正）。結構概念依實際閱讀[Wikipedia「QR code」](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/QR_code)，尺寸與色彩為作者設定。0.05 m tile／0.0006 UV relief（非公尺深度），metallic0。目前目錄893種，含plain的gallery894項。

- `perforatedhardboard`：棕色回火鑽孔硬纖維板，16×16格方陣，每格中心一個圓形暗色不透明孔（約占5.1%面積）與略暗圓角環，孔間為含細纖維grain與碎屑斑的連續棕色板面；非透明通孔，無掛鉤或金屬板。概念依實際閱讀[Wikipedia「Perforated hardboard」](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pegboard)，未引用實物照片或規格，孔距／孔徑／色彩皆作者設定。0.4 m tile／0.004 UV relief（非公尺深度），metallic0。目前目錄892種，含plain的gallery893項。

- `westernredcedar`：北美香柏（Thuja plicata）縱向木面，偏紅金褐色底與細而平行的低對比暗色年輪線，年輪輕微起伏；無邊材、隨機暗帶、節疤或塗層，也無樹脂道。依實際閱讀[Wood Database Western Red Cedar](https://www.wood-database.com/western-red-cedar/)與實看[木面照片](https://www.wood-database.com/wp-content/uploads/thuja-plicata.jpg)近似，僅作配色與年輪線形態參考。重用`douglasfir`年輪sampler，0.12 m tile／0.003 UV relief（非公尺深度），metallic0。目前目錄891種，含plain的gallery892項。

- `chesterfieldleather`：Chesterfield菱格縫釦棕色皮革，6×6格每隔一格中心一顆小釦（每tile 18顆），斜向折線包圍鼓起的菱形軟墊；折線暗且AO低、墊頂較亮較光滑，附細皮革grain與低頻皺紋。作者示意，未引用特定產品或來源；無釦面金屬、縫線或破裂。0.5 m tile／0.012 UV relief（非公尺深度），metallic0。目前目錄890種，含plain的gallery891項。

- `longleafpine`：長葉松縱向木面，淡黃白早材與窄而高對比的橙褐色晚材條紋，年輪近乎平行、輕微起伏；無樹脂道、節疤、整面紅褐心材或塗層。依實際閱讀[Wood Database Longleaf Pine](https://www.wood-database.com/longleaf-pine/)與實看[木面照片](https://www.wood-database.com/wp-content/uploads/longleaf-pine.jpg)近似，僅作配色與條紋形態參考。重用`douglasfir`年輪sampler，0.12 m tile／0.003 UV relief（非公尺深度），metallic0。目前目錄889種，含plain的gallery890項。

- `douglasfir`：Douglas-fir弦切縱向面，淺橙褐色早材與較暗橙褐色晚材年輪構成多重尖拱與同心橢圓；無節疤、樹脂囊、邊／心材分界、塗層，也不是徑切平行年輪。依實際閱讀[Wood Database Douglas-Fir](https://www.wood-database.com/douglas-fir/)與實看[弦切樣板照片](https://www.wood-database.com/wp-content/uploads/pseudotsuga-menziesii.jpg)近似，僅作配色與年輪形態參考。專用週期C1年輪sampler，0.2 m tile／0.0035 UV relief（非公尺深度），metallic0。目前目錄888種，含plain的gallery889項。

- `blackwalnut`：深巧克力褐色黑胡桃木弦切縱向面，較暗長縱紋、直到略起伏的紋理與稀疏短小開放導管凹點；非紫／灰偏色、邊材、捲紋／瘤／鳥眼figure或塗層，也無半環孔導管大小漸變。依實際閱讀[Wood Database Black Walnut](https://www.wood-database.com/black-walnut/)與實看[弦切整板掃描](https://www.wood-database.com/wp-content/uploads/juglans-nigra-full.jpg)近似，僅作配色與紋理方向參考。0.16 m tile／0.0025 UV relief（非公尺深度），metallic0。目前目錄887種，含plain的gallery888項。

- `to92packagefaces`：2×2四個旋轉／微移的TO-92示意：霧黑D形本體（直側、平底、上半橢圓穹頂）、三支等間距平行直腳（寬根肩／窄尾），無金屬片、孔或印字。腳M1，本體／背底M0，C1混合；高度背底.02 < 腳.44 < 本體.68。尺寸僅參考實際下載並實看[onsemi 98ASB42022B，CASE29-11 Issue AM，2007-03-09，PDF第6頁／圖面第1頁](https://www.onsemi.com/pdf/datasheet/p2n2222a-d.pdf#page=6)，非精確CAD，不推定成分或電氣特性。0.08 m tile／0.0042 UV relief（非公尺深度），default metallic1；explicit0只改factor不改mask。建議512，32僅粗預覽。目前目錄886種，含plain的gallery887項。

- `rcafemalefaces`：RCA／Cinch母座正面示意——四個寬圓黑墊圈、較小圓金屬肩、再小的六平面前端筒、暗色絕緣環與凹入母座環／不透明暗底；無BNC卡口凸耳，不把大型後螺帽當正面。中性灰省略真實金光學，形狀／深度／色彩為作者近似；是接頭家族變體，不是電氣／音訊／RF／插合／EMC／安全認證。當時目錄為879種。
- `rj45femalefaces`：RJ45型8P8C母座正面示意——四個薄矩形金屬屏蔽框、霧黑絕緣內襯與低主腔，一側有較窄的階梯卡扣延伸、對側單排八個獨立長金屬接點。無LED；中性灰省略鍍金光學，作者佈局不是精確CAD，不模擬Ethernet／PoE／Cat／速度／電氣／EMC或認證。
- `usbcfemalefaces`：USB-C母座正面示意——四個圓角扁平金屬口、低霧黑腔與中央薄絕緣舌，每面兩排各12個金屬接點（共24）；不同於既有四接點矩形插座。中性灰近似，不模擬鍍金光學、實體空腔、電氣、USB速度／PD或安規認證。
- `iecc6inlets`：IEC C6公入口正面示意——四片平坦黑PA6法蘭，各有三葉形共腔、上單／下雙三個圓金屬腳端與雙沉頭安裝孔；腳端低於法蘭，沒有C8凸護罩，不是C5母孔、C14扁腳或市電／安規模擬。
- `iecc8inlets`：IEC C8公入口正面示意——四片黑PA6法蘭、膠囊形護罩與上下圓角V形縮口的八字形低腔，各有兩個高於腔底、低於護罩的圓金屬腳端；不是C7母孔、三片C14腳或市電／安規模擬。
- `din5femalefaces`：標準DIN五孔母座正面示意——四片非金屬黑PBT法蘭，各有三安裝孔、凸圓口、上鍵形缺口／下連接肋與五個中央膨大長形母孔；孔內低底為opaque stand-in，不是mini-DIN、真孔或MIDI／電氣模擬。
- `xlrfemalefaces`：XLR三孔母座正面示意——四片D尺寸圓角矩形金屬法蘭、左上／右下雙安裝孔、凸圓口、黑芯、上方卡扣與開口內環；上二／下一凹孔的低底為opaque stand-in，不是公針、真孔或音訊／電氣模擬。
- `bncfemalefaces`：BNC母座正面示意——四個低金屬法蘭、高圓筒口、對向雙卡榫及白絕緣環，中心有簡化金屬母接點環與opaque低底；中性灰PBR，不是凸公針、實體孔或射頻模擬。
- `iecc14inlets`：IEC C14公入口正面示意——四個黑色鍵形護罩內，各有上方一片、下方兩片的實心金屬腳端；高於低腔底而低於保護凸緣，不是C13母孔、背端帶孔端子或市電模擬。
- `dsubsocketfaces`：DE-9母座正面示意——四片金屬法蘭、封閉凸D框、黑色絕緣芯與五／四排九個凹入插孔；不是前凸金針、背面焊杯、透明真孔或可連接電路。
- `topadjusttrimmers`：上調式微調電位器頂面示意——四個非金屬矩形外殼，各有偏角小圓形金屬螺絲與單一全寬直線盲槽；槽底仍金屬，不是通孔、中央大螺絲或可調電路模擬。
- `crownprobetips`：四點皇冠探針端面示意——九個圓形金屬面各有四個圓化凸峰、低谷與較低金屬杯；不是十字螺絲凹槽、齒輪、城堡螺帽、真通孔或彈簧／電學模擬。
- `piezodiaphragms`：壓電振膜表面示意——四塊平坦金屬外板各有象牙白陶瓷露邊與單一中央金屬電極；不是陶瓷通孔、喇叭錐、鏡片、分割回授電極或電路／振動／音訊模擬。
- `castellatedboards`：PCB板邊半孔示意——四塊深綠非金屬板各有四排七個向板外開放的凹口與淡銀金屬半環land；不是封閉圓via、QFN、城堡螺帽、真幾何孔或PCB製造／電熱認證。
- `qfnlandpads`：QFN四邊平接點與中央裸焊盤示意——四塊深色非金屬底面，各有四排七個銀色平接點與分離中央盤；不是BGA圓頂、PCB熱via、pinout／必定接地或尺寸／熱電認證。
- `radiolariandiscs`：放射蟲骨盤赤道剖面示意——四個象牙白同心環肋骨架，各有中央腔、6／12／18格環帶與六短棘；全不透明非金屬，不是篩板外殼、蜘蛛網、真通孔或物種／顯微校準。
- `bgasolderballs`：完整BGA焊球矩陣示意——四塊深色方形非金屬載板各有13×13個等距銀色金屬圓頂與接觸陰影；不是環形滾珠、球鏈、pinout／JEDEC尺寸／錫合金或reflow認證。
- `pigskinleather`：三孔群豬皮示意——暖褐霧面皮紋上64組不規則三孔群，每孔圓潤、暗凹且遮蔽；全不透明非金屬，不是單一三角孔、鴕鳥隆圈、人工等大穿孔或皮料真偽認證。
- `trianglesocketheads`：TA 三角盲槽圓頭——25個銀灰鋼頭各有三條直邊的三角凹槽與封閉平坦金屬槽底，置於粗灰非金屬底；不是Y槽、曲邊TP3、真通孔或尺寸／防拆認證。
- `diatomvalves`：舟形矽藻外殼面示意——四片象牙白披針形殼面，兩側細長孔列圍住平齊中央留白，雙raphe中央斷開、末端向同側微彎；全不透明非金屬，不是真通孔、完整雙殼細胞或顯微尺度校準。
- `chitonshells`：石鱉八殼板覆瓦示意——九組橢圓背甲各有八片隆起殼板、七道順向覆瓦接合與粗暗外套膜帶；暖褐、不透明非金屬，不是可動殼片mesh、捲曲或生物力學模型。
- `leafstomata`：葉片氣孔守衛細胞示意——25組腎形細胞隆起圍住深色凹孔，兩端保留稍低的接合；綠色不透明非金屬圖樣，不是真通氣孔、完整葉表皮重建或動態蒸散模型。
- `watermelontourmaline`：西瓜碧璽三角切片鑲板——16片圓角三角的粉紅核心／淺灰過渡帶／綠色外圈共用拋光平面，置於粗灰底；正面不透明色盤近似，沒有透射、折射、多色性或寶石認證。
- `cuttlebone`：烏賊骨層板波壁示意切面——不等高層板與錯位、雙向細波壁圍住深色凹底；暖白粗糙非金屬、全不透明，不是三維開放迷宮重建、真正孔洞或生物力學模型。
- `pallasite`：拋光橄欖隕鐵——橄欖綠／蜜黃非金屬晶粒與連通銀灰鐵鎳基質近乎平齊，晶粒內有細暗裂紋；不透明正面PBR近似，沒有背光透射、精確合金光學或隕石認證。
- `gosperinlay`：三階Gosper黃銅嵌線——粗糙海軍藍非金屬地上的343段等長金屬線，只有兩個圓端點、沒有分岔；有限開放曲線，不是Moore封閉凹槽、極限填充集合或實體嵌線mesh。
- `vogelspiral`：黃金角葉序圓片鑲板——97片等大深青／鼠尾草／象牙平頂圓磚，在粗灰底上形成半步徑向偏移的離散Vogel圖樣；不是連續螺旋線、相切Doyle圓或植物模擬。
- `langtonsant`：蘭頓蟻1024步快照棋磚——32×32淺暖格板上的122個深色亮面黑格，奶油凸三角標出終點朝向；白左黑右的固定作者快照，不是QR碼或即時演化模擬。
- `pentominotiles`：十二種自由五連方釉面拼磚——每塊五格、每個6×10區塊十二形各一；全圖180塊海軍藍／灰藍／象牙拼磚，粗灰縫且同塊內無格線。固定作者佈局，不是互動拼圖或正五邊形磁磚。
- `pythagorastree`：有限畢氏樹釉面方磚——四棵由深赤褐逐代變象牙色的分枝方形樹，各31片、五尺寸層級；灰縫上的非金屬圖樣，不是雙尺寸平面鋪磚、無限碎形或真樹。
- `ichinghexagrams`：六十四卦浮雕爻紋——奶油粗底上的深色實／斷六爻，64種各六行、共576個平頂圓肩長條；作者二進位排列，非文王卦序、占卜或字型。
- `apollonianmosaic`：Apollonian相切圓馬賽克——青綠／鼠尾草／象牙釉面圓盤，以兩輪Descartes反射在每個圖樣填入19個大小不等的相切圓；粗糙灰縫上的有限、不透明非金屬作者圖樣，不是無限碎形或施工規格。
- `prismaticpentagonaltiles`：稜柱房形五角（V3.3.3.4.4）鑲嵌——藍灰／灰綠／象牙上下交錯房形釉面磚，兩個相鄰90°底角與三個120°屋頂角；粗糙灰縫上的不透明非金屬作者近似，無尺寸或施工認證。
- `floretpentagonaltiles`：花形五角（V3.3.3.3.6）手性鑲嵌——森林綠／橄欖／象牙釉面五角磚，六瓣繞一點，每瓣一個60°與四個120°角；粗糙灰縫上的不透明非金屬作者近似，無尺寸或施工認證。
- `snubtrihexagonaltiles`：扭稜三六角（3.3.3.3.6）手性鑲嵌——靛藍正六角與象牙／棕黃三角釉面磁磚，四個三角形與一個六角形在每頂點相遇；粗糙灰縫上的不透明非金屬作者近似，無實際尺寸或施工認證。
- `deltoidaltrihexagonaltiles`：鳶形三六角（V3.4.6.4）鑲嵌磁磚——勃艮第紅、玫瑰與象牙三色釉面鳶形磚，嵌在粗糙灰縫上，每個六角胞被六條通向邊中點的輻條切成六塊鳶形；不透明非金屬作者近似，不含實際磁磚尺寸或鋪貼規格認證。（不同於既有`kisrhombilletiles`的30-60-90三角形與`trihexagonaltiles`的六邊形加三角形。）
- `soroban`：七棒算盤——木製框條與隔條之間，七根竹棒上的雙錐木珠依序顯示4567890（取自頁面圖說1234567890的末七位），隔條上每第三根棒有一個點；不透明非金屬作者近似，不含實際算盤尺寸、材料或教學正確性認證。（不同於既有`seedbeads`的種子珠。）
- `ean13label`：EAN-13條碼標籤——淺色紙面上印著Wikipedia頁面範例號碼4 003994 155486的30條黑色條碼，起始、中央與結尾護線較長；不透明非金屬作者近似，不含GS1規格、掃描器可讀性或印刷品質認證。（不同於既有`papertape`的打孔紙帶。）
- `embossedbraille`：浮凸點字紙——7×4個點字格的奶油色紙面，以光滑隆起的圓點依序拼出a到z的字母（跳過w，後面留三個空格）；不透明非金屬作者近似，不含紙張材料、點距標準或可讀性認證。（不同於既有`tactilepaving`的規則截錐警示凸點。）
- `rule30`：Rule 30元胞自動機——33×33格的tile顯示Wolfram初等元胞自動機Rule 30從頂列中央單一黑格長出的33代（時間向下），黑格是凸起、光滑的深色方塊，白格是較低、較粗糙的淡色方塊，格間有凹陷細溝；不透明非金屬作者近似，不含材料、尺寸規格或隨機性認證。（不同於既有`sierpinskicarpet`、`perfectmaze`等圖樣。）
- `perfectmaze`：種子完美迷宮——8×8格的迷宮，凹陷、深色、較光滑的通道夾在淡色、較粗糙的凸牆之間，64格以63條連結相連，每格都到得了且沒有迴圈，每個seed是不同的迷宮；不透明非金屬作者近似，不含材料、尺寸規格或難度保證。（不同於既有`moorecurve`的單一封閉溝槽迴路。）
- `sevensegment`：七段顯示器面板——5×2格的深色玻璃面板顯示0123456789，亮起的琥珀色斜角長條按常見七段圖樣排列，未亮的段保留暗淡殘影；不透明非金屬作者近似，不含LED／LCD元件、發光或標準規格。（不同於既有`chipresistors`的白色103標記。）
- `ulamspiral`：Ulam質數螺旋——15×15格tile以正方形螺旋寫入整數1–225（1在中央），質數所在格有光滑深色凸圓頂，位於較粗糙的淡色非金屬石板與凹陷格線溝上；不透明非金屬作者近似，不含材料、標準或數學認證。（不同於既有`tactilepaving`的規則截錐凸點與`dominoes`的0–6凹點。）
- `papertape`：打孔紙帶——一吋寬、十個0.1吋字元列×九條孔軌的八軌紙帶tile，淡色非金屬紙面上以孔洞編碼ASCII文字「PAPER TAPE」（第8位元為偶同位），另有一列較小的導孔偏向窄邊，孔中露出深色底襯，孔較粗糙；不透明非金屬作者近似，不含材料、紙厚、半透明或標準規格。（不同於既有`punchcard`的2×4卡片與矩形打孔。）
- `moorecurve`：Moore曲線溝槽磁磚——8×8格的淡色非金屬tile上，一條封閉連續的深色凹溝槽穿過每一格（四條旋轉的Hilbert曲線組成的漢米頓迴路），較粗糙；不透明非金屬作者近似，不含材料、雕刻工法或尺寸規格。（不同於既有溝槽、迷宮與磁磚類preset。）
- `sierpinskicarpet`：Sierpinski地毯石磚——81×81格tile上四層遞迴凹方（1個27格、8個9格、64個3格、512個1格），越大越深越暗，tile可無縫重複；不透明非金屬作者近似，是有限四層近似而非極限集合，不含石種、雕刻工法或尺寸規格。（不同於既有穿孔、嵌板與點蝕類preset。）
- `chladnifigure`：Chladni圖形——深色較光滑的非金屬板上，淡色細沙沿駐波節線聚集，沙略凸起且較粗糙，節線圖樣與UV tile精確重複；不透明非金屬作者近似，不模擬實體板模態、材料或沙粒物理。（不同於既有沙、石與穿孔板類preset。）
- `kisrhombilletiles`：V4.6.12 kisrhombille鑲嵌——每個六邊形被切成十二個全等30-60-90直角三角形，深青色與奶油色釉面三角形繞中心交替，所有直邊落在相隔30°的六組平行線上，邊緣有窄斜邊並被粗糙灰色填縫圍出；不透明非金屬作者近似，不含磁磚厚度、釉料化學或尺寸規格。（不同於既有`rhombille`、3.6.3.6六角三角與等腰直角`righttriangletiles`磁磚，並是`truncatedtrihexagonaltiles`的對偶。）
- `truncatedtrihexagonaltiles`：4.6.12截角三六角鑲嵌——奶油色釉面十二邊形、三個方向各差60°的靛藍小正方形與薰衣草色正六邊形，邊緣有窄斜邊並被粗糙灰色填縫圍出；不透明非金屬作者近似，不含磁磚厚度、釉料化學或尺寸規格。（與既有3.12.12十二邊形、六角三角及`rhombitrihexagonaltiles`磁磚的頂點類型不同。）
- `elongatedtriangulartiles`：3.3.3.4.4長三角鑲嵌——赤陶色直立正方形列與奶油色朝上／朝下正三角形列交替、正方形列錯開半邊，邊緣有窄斜邊並被粗糙灰色填縫圍出；不透明非金屬作者近似，不含磁磚厚度、釉料化學或尺寸規格。（頂點內容與`snubsquaretiles`同為三三角兩正方形但順序不同，且不同於既有六角三角磁磚。）
- `rhombitrihexagonaltiles`：3.4.6.4菱形三六角鑲嵌——奶油色釉面六邊形、三個方向各差60°的石板藍正方形與海軍藍正三角形，邊緣有窄斜邊並被粗糙灰色填縫圍出；不透明非金屬作者近似，不含磁磚厚度、釉料化學或尺寸規格。（與既有`snubsquaretiles`、六角三角及3.12.12十二邊形磁磚的頂點類型不同。）
- `snubsquaretiles`：3.3.4.3.4扭稜正方形鑲嵌——±15°交替旋轉的深藍釉面正方形與奶油色釉面正三角形，邊緣有窄斜邊並被粗糙灰色填縫圍出；不透明非金屬作者近似，不含磁磚厚度、釉料化學或尺寸規格。（與既有六角三角、Cairo五邊形及直角三角形磁磚的頂點類型不同。）
- `starfish`：粗糙灰砂底上的橘色五臂海星，圓潤中央圓盤接五條由根到尖逐漸變窄的臂，臂角與臂長略不規則，臂上有隆起脊線與三列小瘤點；不透明非金屬作者近似，不含物種辨識、管足、棘刺、篩板、真實尺寸或海洋生物學數據。
- `fishscaleshingles`：8層各12個半圓下緣瓣的米色魚鱗金屬瓦，每片兩瓣、逐層錯半瓣、上層疊壓並在下層留陰影，片間有窄縫；不透明非金屬作者近似，不含壓花紋理、金屬反射、真實疊壓幾何或規格認證。（與既有`scallopedslate`、`fishscaletiles`同屬扇貝疊壓家族的變體。）
- `waffleweave`：14×16個固定格的冷灰白圓脊圍出內凹小方格，橫向脊較高、脊交點隆起成小結；不透明非金屬作者近似，不含真實經緯交織、織物物理、吸水性或織物規格認證。（與既有`wafflecloth`同屬華夫格織物家族的較細變體。）
- `halite`：粗糙淡灰粉鹽底上的乳白立方hopper晶面，外緣為隨機旋轉的正方形，內含兩層同心逐級下陷的正方階面；不透明非金屬作者近似，不含鹽晶尺寸量測、透明／溶蝕物理、完整立方體mesh或礦物認證。（與既有`bismuthhoppers`同屬旋轉巢狀方形hopper家族的非金屬鹽晶變體。）
- `calcite`：深褐粗糙礦底上的乳白斜方形平板，兩組平行邊約75°並含巢狀平行階面；不透明非金屬作者近似，不含解理角量測、透明／雙折射、完整菱面體mesh或礦物認證。
- `flangenuts`：圓形錐面法蘭上的削角六角螺帽，含六個直平側與開放圓孔、露出深色粗糙鋼底；整張map為金屬，不含底面鋸齒、完整3D、內螺紋或DIN／ISO認證。
- `capnuts`：深色粗糙鋼底上的六角蓋形螺帽，含六個直平側、削角角隅與封閉圓形球冠；整張map為金屬，不是Hex Nuts的貫穿孔，也不含完整3D、內螺紋或DIN／ISO認證。
- `bedfordcord`：卡其色經向織入凸棱、連續細溝與相鄰半距交錯的扁平緯向短棱；不透明非金屬表面近似，不含真實經緯／襯紗交織、布料物理或棉布規格／來源認證。
- `staurolite`：淺色粗礦底上的深褐60°交叉柱狀雙晶、平行直側與鈍端；四臂共一頂面、不堆疊交心，為不透明非金屬近似，不含完整mesh、折射、片岩片理或礦物／化學認證。
- `dowelpins`：銀色無頭實心圓柱定位銷側影、一端圓弧與對端導入倒角、淺圓柱冠面；鋼／不透明非金屬底分離，不含完整mesh、壓入物理、負載或DIN／ISO認證。
- `europeanbeech`：淡粉褐細直紋歐洲山毛櫸、密集均勻淺凹縱孔與短小平齊射線斑；不含木孔mesh、木種鑑定、防水、結構或來源認證。
- `habotai`：珍珠白細密1/1平織絲綢、同色經緯、低起伏與柔和光澤；選不透明表面近似，不含透光、皺褶／毛絲mesh、布料物理或纖維／重量認證。
- `azurite`：深藍色寬斜端板柱晶面、淺縱紋與局部平行三片晶群，高於深色粗礦底；opaque dielectric不是金屬，不含完整晶柱、透明折射或礦物／含銅安全認證。
- `whiteoak`：暖橄欖褐徑切白橡木，寬長橫向射線斑與直木紋平齊、縱導管較淺；不是凸木瘤，不含木種鑑定、防水或結構性能認證。
- `clevispins`：銀色雙肩短凸頭連接銷側影、無螺紋直軸淺圓冠與對端單封閉圓孔，鋼面／不透明非金屬孔底分離；不含卡簧裝配、完整通孔mesh、負載或DIN／ISO認證。
- `whipcord`：深灰單條陡斜粗脊、同色經緯紗與連續三pick長浮紗；有別於細Gabardine、雙脊Cavalry Twill和橫向羅緞，不含毛羽mesh、衣料物理或紡織性能認證。
- `vanadinite`：橙褐凸六角晶緣與較小同向凹六角平底，散布於淡色粗礦底；不同於實心平頂晶面，不含完整晶柱、透明折射或礦物／含鉛安全認證。
- `tabwashers`：銀色平坦圓環、封閉圓孔與單一向外直端平舌，metal 鋼面／非金屬孔底分離；不是開口、球面或杯形墊圈，不含彎舌、裝配或防鬆性能認證。
- `whiteash`：淺暖褐白蠟木直紋與平齊生長帶，較粗且密集的早材長導管對比細疏晚材孔；只有導管凹入，不含端面圓孔、凸木肋或木種／物性認證。
- `gibheadkeys`：銀色實心鉤頭錐鍵的平坦側視輪廓，含外端斜削鉤頭、單側階差與近1:100直條鍵身；不是 parallelkeys 雙圓端或 Woodruff 半月鍵，不含完整3D鍵、配合、拔出或標準符合認證。
- `grosgrain`：灰藍羅緞細密經線與單條圓凸橫肋，逐根交錯並向兩側平滑下降；不是 Faille 平棱或 Ottoman 成對棱，不含帶邊 mesh／透明／布料物理。
- `pyromorphite`：黃綠磷氯鉛礦平坦六角凸端面與淡色粗礦底，含不同大小／方向；只近似晶柱端面，不含完整稜柱／桶狀晶體、透明光學或礦物／含鉛安全認證。
- `kingwood`：深紅褐磨光心材與不等寬平齊紫黑細縱紋，稀疏導管略凹；不是奶油粉紅 Tulipwood、凸木肋或木種／合法來源認證。
- `faille`：象牙白平紋橫棱織物，細密經紗逐根交錯寬平單緯棱，含交叉起伏與凹紗隙；不是 Ottoman 成對粗棱，不含透布或垂墜物理。
- `celestine`：淡藍平坦寬板天青石，局部平行晶片、錯開端部與中性粗礦底；維持opaque非金屬，不新增透明折射或礦物認證。
- `braziliantulipwood`：奶油底粉紅紅不等寬平齊縱紋的磨光巴西鬱金香木，稀疏導管略凹；不是美洲鵝掌楸、凸木肋或木種／合法來源認證。
- `batiste`：象牙白細棉一上一下平織，均衡同色經緯與細緻normal／height、凹紗隙；維持不透明，不含透光、布片物理或纖維認證。
- `wulfenite`：橙色近方形板狀鉬鉛礦，不等大小與方向的平坦凸晶面配深色粗礦底；opaque非金屬，不含透明晶體mesh或礦物／安全認證。
- `lignumvitae`：作者深橄欖褐磨光癒創木，細長木理維持平齊、稀疏短導管略凹；不是粗斑馬條紋，不含木種鑑定、自潤滑物性或合法採購認證。
- `sateen`：作者象牙白五枚緯面棉緞，孤立接結點與連續四紗緯浮長帶細緻normal／height；不是印色斜紋，不新增布片物理、方向性BRDF或纖維認證。
- `parallelkeys`：銀灰雙圓端平鍵，平坦實心頂面、平行側邊與圓弧端部，金屬面與暗色非金屬底分離；不是圓柱、空心環或具DIN／力學認證的零件mesh。
- `smithsonite`：作者蘋果綠葡萄狀菱鋅礦，較亮滑的連生圓頂配較暗粗糙凹隙；opaque非金屬凹凸面，不含透明、折射、晶體mesh或礦物認證。
- `macassarebony`：作者深褐磨光馬卡薩烏木，近黑不等寬縱紋維持平齊、細密稀疏導管略凹；非刻槽或拼板，不含木種、木材物性或合法採購認證。
- `serge`：作者藏青霧面嗶嘰2/2斜紋，同色經緯帶連續短浮長與normal／height凸紋，非印色斜線；不含羊毛毛羽mesh、衣片物理或纖維／制服等級認證。
- `garterstitch`：暖奶油色搓板針，橫向凸脊帶交錯圓弧purl針圈與較低return arcs，實際normal／height而非針法圖印色；不含完整紗線mesh、彈性／垂墜或纖維認證。
- `woodruffkeys`：銀灰色實心半月鍵，直弦與圓弧圍成近乎平坦凸金屬面，深色底保持非金屬；不是圓幣、三角片、有孔墊圈或裝配mesh，不含DIN／材質等級／安全認證。
- `barite`：淡奶油色、局部平行三板的平坦重晶石晶面，凸於棕色磨砂礦底；不是細長藍色Kyanite凸脊、圓帽或印色，不提供透明、完整礦簇geometry或礦物／安全認證。
- `surah`：作者暖銅色細絲2/2平衡斜紋，兩軸連續短浮長而非逐點凸珠；非銅金屬，不含布料垂墜、透射、方向性絲光或纖維認證。
- `bocote`：黃金褐底深褐近黑木紋，連續縱紋自然回折成局部閉合眼；深淺平齊、稀疏縱導管凹下，不是凸環、木口圖或木種／性能認證。
- `hemimorphite`：天藍色大小不規則連生圓凸礦殼與淡色凹隙，重疊仍限制在單層；不是水玉印色、規則珠格或金屬，不提供透明／完整標本geometry或礦物認證。
- `monkscloth`：奶油原色4×4粗籃織，四條分離紗線與連續四交織點浮長；不是2×2 Hopsack、印色或Oxford雙紗白guide布，不作成分／密度／洗護認證。
- `bronzite`：深褐磨光石面上的局部多方向青銅細紋，明暗石粒平齊且非金屬；僅靜態外觀近似，不提供變角閃光／礦種／價值認證。
- `makore`：紅褐Makore木皮，局部淡出的平齊橫向捲曲木紋與稀疏縱向凹導管；不是等強波浪板或端木孔圖，不提供光學／木種／力學認證。
- `redberyl`：淡色顆粒流紋岩上的覆盆子紅凸起平坦六角晶面，採端面視角；不是尖石英、六瓣花或印刷，不提供晶柱mesh／折射或礦種價值認證。
- `cottondrill`：暖卡其霧面棉斜紋，同色經緯與連續三緯經浮長；不是平織、籃織、藍白條紋或印線，不認證成分／GSM／耐用／洗護。
- `bluelaceagate`：淡藍磨光Blue Lace Agate，平齊粗細白色細蕾絲成對色帶；不是鈷藍石、同寬條紋或凸白脊，不認證礦種／天然染色／光學或療效。
- `linchpins`：銀色閉環扣插銷，圓線保險環、較粗實心柄與凸頭，金屬實物／介電間隙分區；不是R型銷、平墊圈或印刷，不認證機械尺寸／承載／安全。
- `silknoil`：奶油白霧面絹紡短纖布，交替平織與雙向細小真凸纖維結；不是亮面Shantung、印點或未去膠原絲，不作纖維／洗護認證。
- `bloodwood`：鮮紅細直紋血木與稀疏細短凹導管，色帶不凸起、預設介電；不是橘色Padauk或紅漆，不認證樹種／色彩／老化。
- `cavansite`：暖白粒狀礦物底上的亮藍短針放射晶簇，真凸起、較光滑、介電；不是磨光藍珠或長刺星形，不認證礦種或晶體光學。
- `matelasse`：暖象牙白織成絎縫布，連續凸菱接結、淺蓬內片與交替紗冠；不是車縫虛線或印花，不宣稱纖維／洗護認證。
- **黃心木**：均勻金黃縱切面、低對比細直紋與稀疏淺凹短導管；不是螢光黃漆、凸色帶或端木紋。
- **R型髮夾銷**：單圓拉環、長直腳與彎曲外張短腳；圓線金屬面與dielectric空隙，不是封閉墊圈或平行U腳。
- **床墊條紋布**：海軍藍寬中央條搭兩側細線，奶白緯紗交織的緊密2/1斜紋，不是實心印刷條或凸紋。
- **方鈉石**：顆粒藍紫底與填實白色礦脈；維持平整磨光面，不是金斑、凸線或裂洞。
- **Dalmatian stone**：奶白磨光底上的不規則深綠黑礦物斑；色斑平齊，不是凹洞或凸卵石。
- **紫心木**：曝光後茄紫色直紋，中等texture與稀疏凹入導管；不是螢光或動態變色。
- **黑棕櫚木縱切紋**：棕褐地組織裡細密的深色長纖維束；緞面平整，不是端木圓點、年輪或木節。
- **不鏽鋼 E 型擋圈**：三個內側接觸部、連到外開口的寬翼口袋，以及不透明非金屬背板。
- **遊戲系統**：輸入、物理、導航、場景切換、資源載入、存檔，以及 OPM／取樣音訊。
- **渲染**：WebGPU、WebGL2、Canvas2D；各 backend 的功能不同，詳見[目前支援契約](docs/CURRENT.md)。

API 受到 three.js／PixiJS／Excalibur 啟發，但不是相容替代品。引擎無 npm runtime dependencies；第三方 vendor 隨套件附帶並保留各自授權。

### 先跑範例

需要 **Node.js ≥22.13.0** 與 **pnpm 12.6.0**。在此倉庫根目錄執行：

```sh
npx pnpm@12.6.0 install
npx pnpm@12.6.0 examples
```

瀏覽器會開啟 `http://127.0.0.1:5173/examples/`，可依功能篩選並選擇 backend。若只想啟動開發伺服器，改用 `npx pnpm@12.6.0 dev`。請透過 HTTP／HTTPS 存取，不要直接開啟 `file://`。

### 在自己的專案使用

npm 尚未發佈，請使用 [GitHub Releases](https://github.com/JS-PACKAGE/XYZ.js/releases) 的套件附件，或在本機建置封裝：

```sh
npx pnpm@12.6.0 build
npx pnpm@12.6.0 pack
```

將產生的 `xyz.js-1.22.1.tgz` 複製到你的專案，再於該專案安裝：

```sh
npx pnpm@12.6.0 add ./xyz.js-1.22.1.tgz
```

在可解析 `xyz.js` 的 TypeScript／bundler 專案中，先加入畫布：

```html
<canvas id="game"></canvas>
```

再從入口模組啟動：

```ts
import { Game } from 'xyz.js';

const game = await Game.create({ canvas: '#game', renderer: 'webgpu' });
game.addEventListener('error', (event) =>
  console.error((event as CustomEvent<Error>).detail),
);
game.start();
```

這是既有的 WebGPU triangle 入門範例；不支援 WebGPU 時會報錯。一般遊戲可省略 `renderer`，採預設 `auto`，並使用自己的 `Scene`。暫停、恢復與清理分別使用 `game.pause()`、`game.resume()`、`game.destroy()`。

需要完整專案流程，請看[使用說明](docs/USAGE-zh.md)中的獨立 2D／3D starter、Scene、Sprite 與部署範例。

### 開始前要知道

- **Fallback 只發生在初始化**：`auto` 依 WebGPU → WebGL2 → Canvas2D 嘗試；強制指定 backend 不會偷偷切換。執行中 GPU／GL 遺失預設嘗試同 backend 復原，舊 render targets／snapshots 需重建。
- **Canvas2D 不是完整替代**：僅支援 2D，不支援 3D 或 native shader effects。使用 `game.graphics.capabilities` 判斷功能，不要只看 backend 名稱。
- **音訊需要使用者操作**：在點擊等手勢中呼叫 `await game.audio.unlock()`。`game.pause()` 不會自動暫停音訊。WebGPU／AudioWorklet 需要安全來源，localhost 可用，正式部署使用 HTTPS；OPM 無法啟動時取樣／串流音訊仍可用，播放 OPM 前請確認 `game.audio.opmState === 'ready'`。
- **部署要保留完整產物**：無 bundler 時複製整個 `dist/`，包含 `dist/vendor/opm/`、其他 vendor、workers／worklets 與授權檔；不要只拿入口 JS。不要混用 CommonJS 與 ESM 建立的引擎物件。可選的 `opmModuleUrl` 能改從以 commit 釘住的 CDN 載入 OPM，但無法驗證內容完整性（見技術參考）。
- **支援不等於實機認證**：managed WebKit 不是 Safari；模擬輸入、CI 與音訊訊號量測不代表實體手把、手機、可聽輸出或所有 GPU 已驗證。實測範圍與剩餘限制以 [ACCEPTANCE](ACCEPTANCE.md) 為準。

## Examples／範例／サンプル

先從以下範例挑一個；連結指向原始碼目錄，執行時使用開發伺服器的 `/examples/<name>/`。完整清單見[範例目錄](examples/index.html)或[使用說明](docs/USAGE-zh.md)。

| 範例 / Example                           | 看什麼 / Purpose                                   |
| ---------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------- |
| [showcase](examples/showcase/)           | 2D＋3D＋音訊與 Scene 切換 / integrated engine demo |
| [pong](examples/pong/)                   | 小型 2D 遊戲、輸入與計分 / input and scoring       |
| [gameplay2d](examples/gameplay2d/)       | 動畫、物理、地圖與粒子 / 2D gameplay systems       |
| [rendering2d](examples/rendering2d/)     | 遮罩、混合與渲染功能 / 2D rendering                |
| [pbr3d](examples/pbr3d/)                 | 程序式材質、光照與貼圖預覽 / PBR materials         |
| [gltf3d](examples/gltf3d/)               | 模型、骨骼動畫與 morph / model animation           |
| [beacon-run](examples/beacon-run/)       | 載入到存檔、重玩的完整流程 / playable 3D reference |
| [fallback-demo](examples/fallback-demo/) | 切換 backend 與檢查能力 / backend capabilities     |

| 查歷史驗收與未驗事項 / Verification history    | [ACCEPTANCE](ACCEPTANCE.md)                                     |
| 回報安全問題 / Security                        | [Security policy](SECURITY.md)                                  |

常用命令（已安裝 pnpm 12.6.0 時可省略 `npx pnpm@12.6.0`，直接使用 `pnpm`）：

```sh
npx pnpm@12.6.0 build
npx pnpm@12.6.0 typecheck
npx pnpm@12.6.0 test
npx pnpm@12.6.0 lint
npx pnpm@12.6.0 format:check
```

`npx pnpm@12.6.0 docs:api` 產生可搜尋的 API 文件；`npx pnpm@12.6.0 build:site` 建置範例與文件網站至 `.vite/site/`，文件入口為 `docs/`，API 位於 `api/1.22.1/`。部署時保留整個網站目錄，並以支援 directory index 的 HTTP／HTTPS 伺服器提供。

README 只提供入門與導覽；各版本的階段紀錄、測試數與效能量測留在驗收文件，不視為目前版本的通用保證。

## English

XYZ.js is a browser-native TypeScript game engine with 2D/3D rendering, scenes, animation, physics, navigation, UI, input, assets and audio. Use the public `xyz.js` entry; ECS stays internal. It is not a visual editor or a drop-in replacement for three.js, PixiJS or Excalibur. There are no npm runtime dependencies; bundled vendors retain their licenses.

**Start here:** with Node.js ≥22.13.0, run `npx pnpm@12.6.0 install`, then `npx pnpm@12.6.0 examples`. Open `http://127.0.0.1:5173/examples/`. For your own project, install a GitHub release tarball or build and pack locally; npm is unpublished. The [English guide](docs/USAGE.md) covers installation, standalone 2D/3D starters and deployment.

U-bow shackles with raised cross-pins and drilled pin tabs, Ottoman cloth with paired coarse transverse weft ribs, polished ivory howlite with fine gray cellular veins, handmade mulberry paper with Presets support palette `tint`, Worker generation with `workerPool`, reusable `cache`, and optional `heightMap` for parallax.
- **Hopsack**: navy 2×2 basket-weave material with paired yarns, continuous floats and recessed grooves separating individual yarns.
- **Snowflake obsidian**: a polished black face with differently sized, flush gray radial inclusions.
- `f05opticalfaces`: four uncapped JIS-F05 optical receiver faces: matte-black resin rectangles, long side recesses, raised six-sided guides with two chamfered corners at one end and rounded side bosses, dark keyed mouths and low round wells. All dielectric; no shutter, LED or transparent lens. An authored approximation of the NRND TORX1350A(F) horizontal drawing, not optical/data/audio/electrical/fit/EMC/safety simulation or certification. The catalog then had 880 kinds.
- `minidin4femalefaces`: four 2×2 mini-DIN four-contact female faces: black PBT carriers/notched circular inserts, continuous thin tin-plated brass earth rims, open top notches, two lower side steps, upper vertical/lower horizontal slots and separate closed lower key openings. Based on the separate MD-40S front in [Same Sky MD-S page 2](https://www.sameskydevices.com/product/resource/md-s.pdf) and the [manufacturer photo](https://cdn.sameskydevices.com/products/image/getproductimage/976?typecode=m&width=300), not the generic nine-contact drawing or PCB footprints. Only the earth rim is metal; default metallic 1/explicit 0 never changes the stored mask. No outer shielding can, visible silver internal contact annuli or video/electrical simulation. Authored shape/PBR approximations, not CAD/fit/certification. Prefer 512; 32 is coarse. The catalog then had 881 kinds.
- `dcbarreljackfaces`: four 2×2 DC barrel-jack front illustrations: tall black PBT rectangles, upward-offset dark round mouths, solid recessed nickel pins and separate lower silver strips (flat top/bottom, circle-clipped ends only). Only pin/strip have M1; all PBT/floors have M0. Depth is backing < bore < strip < pin < carrier; no outer metal rim or added raised bezel. Based on [Same Sky PJ-102A](https://www.sameskydevices.com/product/interconnect/connectors/dc-power-connectors/jacks/pj-102a), [primary drawing page 2](https://www.sameskydevices.com/product/resource/pj-102a.pdf) and [front photo](https://cdn.sameskydevices.com/products/image/getproductimage/690?typecode=m&width=300). Default metallic 1/explicit 0 changes only the factor, not the stored mask. Rear solder legs, PCB and real through-holes are omitted; no source assets are bundled. An authored front illustration, not CAD/electrical/polarity/switch/safety/fit simulation or certification. Prefer 512; 32 is coarse. The catalog then had 882 kinds.
- `stfiberadapterfaces`: four 2×2 simplified ST simplex fronts: six-flat plated-metal hex bases, concentric round collars, raised circular barrel lips with ONE open top axial keyway and TWO opposed side bayonet studs, and deep dark opaque circular cavities. The keyway lowers ONLY the lip to the continuous metal collar, never the background. Hardware/key floors have M1; cavities/backing M0. Depth: backing < bore < nut < collar/key < studs < lip. Based on [SENKO ST](https://www.senko.com/product/st-adapter-2/), five-page [DS-ST-000006-RevA-EN pages 1/3](https://www.senko.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/01/DS-ST-000006-RevA-EN.pdf) and the [primary photo](https://www.senko.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/09/ST-Adapter.png), using 216-101. Only plated metal is known, not nickel. Internal zirconia is specified but no white ring is clearly exposed; no ceramic ring, center contact or pin is drawn. 29.3 mm is axial depth, double-D 9.6 × 7.8 mm a panel-cutout reference, not the front; metadata creation 2024-11-15 is not a printed date. Authored proportions, not CAD; no threads/knurl teeth/washer/caps/rear/cable/ferrule/PCB or optical/electrical/mating/safety simulation or browser/FPS certification. 0.04 m tile, 0.0042 UV relief, default metallic 1; explicit 0 is factor-only. Constructor caches; no query allocation/loop/hash/trig/sqrt. The catalog then had 883 kinds.
- `ftypefemalefaces`: four 2×2 bare F-type female bulkhead fronts: six-flat nickel-plated metal hex bases, concentric raised round shoulders, CLOSED unkeyed raised circular lips, pale P.P. dielectric disks and tiny deeper dark opaque entries. Exterior hardware M1; backing/P.P./entry M0. Depth: backing < entry < nut < shoulder < P.P. < lip. No ST keyway/studs, male pin or invented visible central contact ring. Based on the historical [Amphenol Connex CAT-103 ©2001, PDF page 159 / printed 158](https://docs.rs-online.com/1ab7/A700000006717073.pdf#page=159), 222121 photo/drawing and Nickel/P.P./75Ω table, not generic Delrin/Teflon. 11 HEX, 21.3 mm axial length and washer/nut side dimensions are references, not measured front height. The actual tiny entry cross-section is unresolved; its circle is an artist simplification. Default 0.04 m tile/0.0042 UV relief (not metre depth), metallic 1; explicit 0 changes only the factor, not the stored mask. No thread helix/decorative thread turns, washer teeth, rear/cable, real holes/transparency/new mesh. Authored material/optical approximations, not CAD/RF/75Ω/electrical/mating/safety simulation; historical 75Ω only identifies the part. Constructor caches; no query allocation/loop/hash/trig/sqrt. Existing pbr3d and texture ownership apply; prefer 512, 32 is coarse preview. The catalog then had 884 kinds.
- `to220packagefaces`: four 2×2 exposed-tab TO-220 fronts with TWO clipped upper tab corners, ONE dark opaque recessed mounting hole, matte-black rectangular bodies and THREE uniformly spaced parallel straight leads (broad root shoulders/narrow tails). Tab/leads M1, other surfaces M0 with C1 blends—not electrical isolation. Authored heights: backing .02 < hole .06 < tab .30 < lead .44 < body .68. Based on [STP55NF06, May 2012, Doc ID7544 Rev11](https://www.st.com/resource/en/datasheet/stb55nf06.pdf#page=13), actually viewed cover page 1/Figure 23 page 13 and read table page 12, not TO-220FP. Dimensions are references, not CAD/heat/electrical/RF/safety/FPS simulation; no chemistry or plating is inferred. Defaults: 0.08 m tile, 0.0042 UV relief (not metres), metallic 1; explicit 0 changes only factor, not mask. Cached typed payload is 73,856 B excluding JS overhead; queries have no allocation/loop/hash/trig/sqrt/neighbor search. Prefer 512; 32 need not resolve narrow leads. Catalog: 885 kinds; gallery including plain: 886.
- `robinsonschensted`: Robinson–Schensted correspondence — for three permutations (identity, reversal, random, random involution, shift, multiplication by 2 mod 11) the permutation matrix, the insertion tableau P and the recording tableau Q (standard Young tableaux of the same shape, numbers 1 to 10), one permutation per row of three panels; magenta on a dark plum ground; seed % 6 picks the type of the first row and ⌊seed / 6⌋ the step between the types and the random keys; an exactly periodic pattern; a schematic picture linking the ideas of `permutations`, `standardtableaux` and `youngdiagrams` (overlapping conceptually); tests read the picture back, recover the permutation by reverse bumping, verify Schensted's theorem (first row = longest increasing subsequence, number of rows = longest decreasing subsequence) and Schützenberger's symmetry (P = Q iff an involution), and compare point by point the ink, height, tone and occlusion; the current catalog has 1182 kinds, 1183 gallery choices including plain.

- `friezegroups`: Frieze groups — eight strips (8 rows each, a 64×64 grid), each a 3×3 glyph with no symmetry repeated by one of the 7 frieze groups (p1, p11g, p1m1, p11m, p2, p2mg, p2mm), one strip of each group plus one of an extra group in a shuffled order; mint on a dark green ground; seed % 6 picks the glyph and ⌊seed / 6⌋ the order and the extra group; an exactly periodic pattern; a schematic picture, the one-dimensional analogue of `wallpapergroups` (the same symmetry-group family); tests try every translation, mirror, half-turn and glide on the picture to find the symmetry group of each strip and check that the seven groups appear once each plus the extra one and their periods, and compare point by point the ink, height, tone and occlusion; the current catalog has 1181 kinds, 1182 gallery choices including plain.

- `huffmancodes`: Huffman codes — the Huffman code and the Shannon–Fano code for the same 16 weights (approximate English letter frequencies, Zipf, Fibonacci numbers, powers of 2, all equal, squares), in two rows of two panels, a symbol per row, a bit 0 dimmer and a 1 brighter; bronze on a dark brown ground; seed % 6 picks the weights of the top row and ⌊seed / 6⌋ those of the bottom row; an exactly periodic pattern; a schematic picture; tests read the picture back and check the prefix property, the Kraft sum of 1, that the Huffman total length is the least (by a separately written merging), that the average length lies between the entropy and the entropy plus 1, that Shannon–Fano is no better than Huffman and the staircase of Fibonacci numbers and powers of 2, and compare point by point the ink, height, tone and occlusion; the current catalog has 1180 kinds, 1181 gallery choices including plain.

- `burrowswheeler`: Burrows–Wheeler transform — the 24 rotations and the sorted rotations of two texts of 24 characters (the Fibonacci word, the Thue–Morse word, mississippi, banana and abracadabra repeated, a random four-letter text), one text per row of two panels, the last column being the transform; lavender on a dark indigo ground; seed % 6 picks the top text and ⌊seed / 6⌋ the bottom text and the key of the random one; an exactly periodic pattern; a schematic picture whose variants 0 and 1 use the same words as `fibonacciword` and `thuemorse` but draw the rotation matrix; tests read the picture back, check the rotations, that the sorted rotations equal a separately written sort by suffix order and that the last column gives back the text through the LF mapping, and compare point by point the ink, height, tone and occlusion; the current catalog has 1179 kinds, 1180 gallery choices including plain.

- `lightsoutpuzzles`: Lights Out puzzles — two puzzles on 5, 6, 7 and 9 boards, each row of two panels holding the board of lit lights and a fewest-press solution (Gaussian elimination over GF(2), taking the lightest of all solutions when there are quiet patterns); a puzzle is made by pressing a pattern (the centre, a cross, every cell, a checkerboard, the border ring or the diagonal) on a dark board; cream on a dark brown ground; seed % 6 picks the pattern of the top puzzle and ⌊seed / 6⌋ the size and the pattern of the bottom one; an exactly periodic pattern; a schematic picture; tests read the picture back, simulate the presses directly to check that every light goes out, list all the solutions by light chasing to check that the solution on the picture has the fewest presses (4 solutions for size 5, 256 for size 9), and compare point by point the ink, height, tone and occlusion; the current catalog has 1178 kinds, 1179 gallery choices including plain.

- `pseudoprimeliars`: Pseudoprime liars — 64×64 rounded squares, the rows odd numbers n (8 windows from 521 to 8,997, the first seven each holding a Carmichael number) and the columns bases a, the cell telling whether a passes the Fermat, Euler, strong (Miller–Rabin) or Euler–Jacobi test for n: shares a factor with n, witness, liar with n prime and liar with n composite (a pseudoprime); ice blue on a dark navy ground; seed % 4 picks the test and ⌊seed / 4⌋ the window and range of bases; an exactly periodic pattern; a schematic picture of the same "(n, a) table" family as `multiplicativeorder` (Fermat liars relate to multiplicative orders) but showing something different; tests read the picture back and compare every cell with a separately written test (big-integer modular powers, trial-division factorisation and a product of Legendre symbols), check that a prime has no witness, strong ⊆ Euler–Jacobi ⊆ Euler ⊆ Fermat liars and that the rows of Carmichael numbers (Korselt) have no Fermat witness, and compare point by point the ink, height, tone and occlusion; the current catalog has 1177 kinds, 1178 gallery choices including plain.

- `finitefieldtables`: Finite field tables — the addition and multiplication tables of GF(4), GF(8), GF(9), GF(16), GF(5) and GF(7), in two rows of two panels (the two rows are the same field with two namings of the elements), the elements named by their polynomial coefficients so that the names 0 and 1 are the zero and the one; gold on a dark navy ground; seed % 6 picks the field and ⌊seed / 6⌋ changes the two shuffles; an exactly periodic pattern; a schematic picture, unlike `cayleytable` (group tables, one operation); tests read the four tables back from the picture and check all the field axioms (identities, commutativity, associativity, distributivity, the Latin square property, inverses, no zero divisors), the characteristic, that the multiplicative group is cyclic and that the two namings have the same distribution of orders, and compare point by point the ink, height, tone and occlusion; the current catalog has 1176 kinds, 1177 gallery choices including plain.

- `minesweeperboards`: Minesweeper boards — a finished game of Minesweeper (9×9, 12×12, 16×16, 8×8), every cell without a mine showing the number of mines around it as pips of a 3×3 dice filled clockwise along the outer ring (0 to 8 pips) and a mine a brighter, higher plate with all nine pips lit; the mines are the cells with the smallest hash keys; coral on a dark maroon ground; seed % 6 picks the board and ⌊seed / 6⌋ changes where the mines are; an exactly periodic pattern; a schematic picture, unlike `gamedice` (thrown dice); tests read the list of mines back from the picture, recompute the numbers another way (adding one to the neighbours of every mine) and compare them with the pips, check the number of mines and that the groups differ, and compare point by point the tone, height and occlusion of the plates and pips; the current catalog has 1175 kinds, 1176 gallery choices including plain.

- `beattysequences`: Beatty sequences — consecutive whole numbers laid out row by row in a w×w grid (24, 32, 34, 40) of rounded squares, the terms of ⌊kα⌋ bright and those of ⌊kβ⌋ (1/α + 1/β = 1) dim, every cell in exactly one by Rayleigh's theorem; the alphas are φ, √2, 1 + √2, √3, √5 and 2 + √3; aqua on a dark teal ground; seed % 6 picks alpha and ⌊seed / 6⌋ the width and start offset; an exactly periodic pattern; a schematic picture whose φ variant is the same kind of picture as `fibonacciword` (not an entirely new concept); tests read the picture back and compare every cell with a separately written test using only whole-number arithmetic and integer square roots, check that every cell is in exactly one sequence and that the first has density about 1/α, and compare point by point the ink, height, tone and occlusion; the current catalog has 1174 kinds, 1175 gallery choices including plain.

- `diophantinegrid`: Diophantine grid — 64×64 rounded squares standing for integer pairs (a, b), lit when a² + b², a² + ab + b², a² − ab + b², (a² + b²)/2, a² + 3b² or a² − b² (a > b) is exactly a square, the primitive solutions (a and b coprime) the brightest; silver on a charcoal ground; seed % 6 picks the form and ⌊seed / 6⌋ moves the window; an exactly periodic pattern; a schematic picture, overlapping with `gcdtable` (also a grid of (a, b)) but drawing only the subset of squares and telling primitive ones apart; tests read the picture back and compare every cell with a separate oracle, check the primitive Pythagorean solutions with Euclid's formula, closure under doubling and symmetry, and compare point by point the ink, height, tone and occlusion; the current catalog has 1173 kinds, 1174 gallery choices including plain.

- `transformbasis`: Transform basis images — the N by N basis images of the two-dimensional cosine transform (DCT-II, N = 4, 5, 6 and 8) and Haar wavelet transform (N = 4 and 8) as N by N panels of N by N rounded pixels, the tone and height following (value + 1)/2, the panels parted by wider gaps; pink on a dark violet ground; seed % 6 picks the variant and the bits of ⌊seed / 6⌋ swap the columns and rows of the panels and change the sign; an exactly periodic pattern; a schematic picture, unlike `walshmatrix` and `zernikemodes`; tests read the value of every pixel back from the picture and compare it with the definition (within 1e-9), check that all the images are mutually orthogonal, their squared norms, that the values of the Haar images are only −1, 0 and 1 and Parseval's identity, and compare point by point the ink, height, tone and occlusion; the current catalog has 1172 kinds, 1173 gallery choices including plain.

- `hanoistates`: Tower of Hanoi states — one panel for every state of the shortest solution (2, 4 or 6 disks to the third or the second peg, 4, 16 or 64 states), the disks on three pegs bars centred on their peg whose width, tone and height follow their size; lime on a dark green ground; seed % 6 picks the variant and the bits of ⌊seed / 6⌋ mirror the pegs and reverse the order of the panels; an exactly periodic pattern; a schematic picture, unlike `graycode`; tests read the stacks of every panel back from the picture and compare them with a separate recursion, check that every state is legal, that the 2 ^ disks states are all different, that the first and last state are on the right pegs, that every step moves a top disk and that the disk moved at step t is 1 plus the number of trailing zeros of t, and compare point by point the ink, height, tone and occlusion; the current catalog has 1171 kinds, 1172 gallery choices including plain.

- `euclideanrhythms`: Euclidean rhythms — the rhythms E(k, n) of k onsets spread as evenly as possible round a circle of n steps, one row per k (k from 1 to n), a step with an onset toned and raised by the gap that follows it (the shorter or the longer) and a step without an onset a faint low tile; n = 8, 12, 16, 20, 24 and 32; amber on a dark brown ground; seed % 6 picks the variant and ⌊seed / 6⌋ turns row k left by group·k steps; an exactly periodic pattern; a schematic picture, unlike `necklaces`; tests read the picture back and check that every row is a rotation of the result of a separately written Bjorklund algorithm, that group 0 equals the formula "the floor of (i + 1)k/n is more than the floor of ik/n", that every row has k onsets with gaps of only two sizes, n mod k longer gaps and onsets in every window of the circle differing by at most 1, and compare point by point the ink, height, tone and occlusion; the current catalog has 1170 kinds, 1171 gallery choices including plain.

- `editdistance`: Edit distance table — the dynamic programming table of two words of the same length (the edit distance or the length of the longest common subsequence; intention / execution, recurrence / occurrence and two 16-letter DNA strings), the tone and height of a cell following its value and the cells of one optimal path back from the last cell bigger and higher; teal on a dark green ground; seed % 6 picks the variant and the bits of ⌊seed / 6⌋ reverse or swap the words; an exactly periodic pattern; a schematic picture, unlike `euclidsteps`; tests read the whole table back from the picture and compare it with a separate recursion, check that every cell fits the recurrence, that a swap gives the transposed table, that the path in the picture equals a separately written walk back and costs the value of the last cell, and compare point by point the ink, height, tone and occlusion; the current catalog has 1169 kinds, 1170 gallery choices including plain.

- `sortingtraces`: Sorting traces — the array of 64 values after every step of a sorting algorithm, one row per step, the tone and height of a cell following its value: bubble, insertion, selection, bottom-up merge, quick (Lomuto) and heap sort; rose on a dark plum ground; seed % 6 picks the algorithm and ⌊seed / 6⌋ the input (an arithmetic progression mod 64); an exactly periodic pattern; a schematic picture, unlike the data-independent `sortingnetwork`; tests read the whole 64×64 back from the picture and compare it cell by cell with a separately written trace, check what each algorithm guarantees at every step (the sorted tail of bubble sort, the sorted prefix of insertion sort, the least values of selection sort, the sorted runs of merge sort, the non-decreasing number of pivots in place of quicksort, the max-heap and sorted tail of heapsort), and compare point by point the ink, height, tone and occlusion; the current catalog has 1168 kinds, 1169 gallery choices including plain.

- `golombrulers`: Golomb rulers — optimal Golomb rulers (every distance between two marks different, the least length; 5 to 10 marks, lengths 11, 17, 25, 34, 44 and 55, found by a search I wrote myself): a cell (i, j) has a dot when both i and j are marks, so every diagonal beside the main one has at most one dot; the tone and height follow the difference |i − j|; gold on a dark brown ground; seed % 6 picks the variant and ⌊seed / 6⌋ one of all the optimal rulers (both orientations) of that number of marks; an exactly periodic pattern; a schematic picture, unlike `costasambiguity`, `projectiveplane` and `nimproduct`; tests read the marks back from the picture, compare them with a separately written search (up to 9 marks), check that the differences are all different and the number of dots on every diagonal, and compare point by point the ink, height, tone and occlusion; the current catalog has 1167 kinds, 1168 gallery choices including plain.

- `langfordpairings`: Langford pairings — sequences in which every number from 1 to n stands twice with k numbers between the two ends of k (Langford pairings: all 2 of n = 3 and of n = 4, all 52 of n = 7) or with the two ends k places apart (Skolem sequences: all 6 of n = 4, all 10 of n = 5 and the first 64 of the 504 of n = 8 in the lexicographic order); every sequence is a panel and every number a bar, the tone and height following the number; mint on a dark green ground; seed % 6 picks the variant and the bits of ⌊seed / 6⌋ reverse the order of the panels and the reading of the sequences; an exactly periodic pattern; a schematic picture, unlike `permutations`, `golombruns` and `setpartitions`; tests read the numbers back from the picture, compare them with an enumeration written another way, check the distance of the two ends of every number, that the panels are all different and the number of them in a complete variant, and compare point by point the ink, height, tone and occlusion; the current catalog has 1166 kinds, 1167 gallery choices including plain.

- `mondrianpartitions`: Mondrian partitions — a square cut into integer rectangles no two of which are congruent, the tone and height following the area; the partitions of 5×5, 6×6, 7×7, 8×8 and 10×10 with the least spread of the areas (4, 5, 5, 6 and 8, found by a search I wrote myself, the least value not guaranteed for n = 10) and a perfect squared square of side 112 (21 squares of different sizes, Duijvestijn's); blue on a dark navy ground; seed % 6 picks the variant and ⌊seed / 6⌋ mod 8 the turn or reflection; an exactly periodic pattern; a schematic picture, unlike `euclidsteps`, `pentominotiles` and `trominotiling`; tests read every block back from the picture (parted by gap lines) and check that it is a rectangle, the sum of the areas, that all shapes differ, the spread of the areas, the tone rising with the area, that the 21 sides equal the list, the turns and reflections of the groups and the edge smoothing, and compare point by point the ink, height, tone and occlusion; the current catalog has 1165 kinds, 1166 gallery choices including plain.

- `debruijntorus`: De Bruijn torus — a grid of symbols that wraps round its edges in which every possible window pattern appears exactly once (a perfect map): 2×2 windows of 2 symbols on 4×4, 2×3 windows of 2 symbols on 8×8 and 4×16, 3×2 windows of 2 symbols on 8×8 and 2×2 windows of 3 symbols on two 9×9 grids; pale violet rounded squares on a dark violet ground, the tone and height following the symbol; seed % 6 picks the variant and ⌊seed / 6⌋ moves the grid and changes its symbols; an exactly periodic pattern; a schematic picture whose grids were found by a backtracking search I wrote myself; tests read the symbols back from the picture, check that every window is unique, that the smaller windows are balanced, that the turned windows are no longer unique, the move and symbol change by group, and compare point by point the ink, height, tone and occlusion; the current catalog has 1164 kinds, 1165 gallery choices including plain.

- `kneser`: Kneser matrix — every k-subset of n elements has a row and a column, the cells are rounded squares whose tone and height follow the number of elements two subsets share (the Johnson scheme); disjoint ones (the edges of the Kneser graph) are the brightest and the diagonal the darkest; (n, k) is (5,2), (6,2), (7,2), (6,3), (7,3) or (8,3); amber on a dark brown ground; seed % 6 picks the variant and ⌊seed / 6⌋ mod 3 the order (lexicographic, sum of the elements, revolving door); an exactly periodic pattern; a schematic picture, unlike the 0/1 incidence matrix of `projectiveplane`; tests compute every shared count by another method, check that the matrix is symmetric, that the count of every share in a row is a binomial coefficient, that the intersection numbers of the association scheme depend only on the share, that seed 0 is the Petersen graph (strongly regular (10,3,0,1)), that every 3-subset of 6 has exactly its complement as a disjoint partner and that successive revolving-door rows share k−1 elements, and compare point by point the ink, height, tone and occlusion; the current catalog has 1163 kinds, 1164 gallery choices including plain.

- `lowdiscrepancy`: Low-discrepancy points — the first points of a low-discrepancy sequence on the torus, one dot each: 144 points of the Halton sequence (bases 2, 3), 64 of the Hammersley set, 144 of the Fibonacci lattice, 144 of the Kronecker sequence of the plastic number, 80 of the Halton sequence (bases 2, 5) and 75 of it (bases 3, 5); the tone and height of a dot follow its index in the sequence (so one sees how the sequence fills the square); cream on a dark navy ground; seed % 6 picks the variant and ⌊seed / 6⌋ the group, which moves the whole set on the torus; an exactly periodic pattern; a schematic picture, unlike the random `poissondisk` and the golden-angle `vogelspiral`; tests compute every point again by separate formulas, check that the height at the middle of every dot is that of its index and that there is no extra or missing point on a grid, that every box of the Halton and Hammersley sets holds exactly one point and that the Fibonacci lattice is closed under addition, and compare point by point the ink, height, tone and occlusion; the current catalog has 1162 kinds, 1163 gallery choices including plain.

- `hamiltonpaths`: Hamiltonian paths — every path along the edges of a small grid of points that visits every point exactly once (walked either way counting once) drawn once: the 8, 20, 62, 132, 276 and 336 of the grids 3×2, 3×3, 4×3, 5×3, 4×4 and 6×3 points; the two ends carry bigger dots, and the tone and height of a path follow its number of turns; coral on a dark brown-black ground; seed % 6 picks the variant and ⌊seed / 6⌋ the order of the panels; an exactly periodic pattern; a schematic picture, unlike `hamiltoncycle` (one random big cycle) and `selfavoidingwalks` (walks of a fixed number of steps); tests read the path of every panel back from the picture, check that each is one path through every point and that there are no repeats, check that the number drawn equals the number of Hamiltonian paths counted by dynamic programming, and compare point by point the ink, height, tone and occlusion; the current catalog has 1161 kinds, 1162 gallery choices including plain.

- `gridsplits`: Grid splits — every way to split a grid board into two regions that are each in one piece drawn once: the 19, 70, 85 and 245 splits of the boards 4×3, 4×4, 6×3 and 5×4 into two equal halves, the 42 of 4×4 into 6 and 10 cells and the 36 of 5×3 into 7 and 8; the cells of a region join without a seam and the region of the top left cell is brighter and higher; light green on a dark green-black ground; seed % 6 picks the variant and ⌊seed / 6⌋ the order of the panels; an exactly periodic pattern; a schematic picture, unlike `dominoboards` (domino tilings) and `polyominoes` (shapes); tests read the split of every panel back from the picture, check the size of the region of the top left cell, that both regions are in one piece and that there are no repeats, compare the set with an enumeration that grows connected sets from the top left cell and keeps those whose other cells are connected too, and compare point by point the ink, height, tone and occlusion; the current catalog has 1160 kinds, 1161 gallery choices including plain.

- `chorddiagrams`: Chord diagrams — every chord diagram of one kind (points on a circle joined in pairs by straight chords) drawn once: all 15 and 105 of 6 and 8 points, the 14 and 42 with no crossing of 8 and 10 points (Catalan numbers) and the 18 and 105 classes under turning of 8 and 10 points; the tone and height of dots and chords follow the number of crossings; silver-white on a dark charcoal ground; seed % 6 picks the variant and ⌊seed / 6⌋ the order of the panels and the turn of the classes; an exactly periodic pattern; a schematic picture, unlike `polygondissections` (a polygon cut into pieces); tests read the diagram and level of every panel back from the picture, check that each is of the kind and that there are no repeats, compare the set with an enumeration that goes through all the sets of pairs of points, and compare point by point the ink, height, tone and occlusion; the current catalog has 1159 kinds, 1160 gallery choices including plain.

- `stilllifes`: Still lifes — every still life (a pattern the rules of Conway's Game of Life leave unchanged) in a small box drawn once: all 11, 27, 82 and 91 in boxes of 3 by 3, 3 wide by 4 high, 4 by 4 and 6 wide by 3 high, and the 74 and 69 of those in 4 by 4 and 6 by 3 whose live cells are joined; a live cell is a flat tile whose tone and height follow its number of live neighbours (2 lower, 3 higher); sky blue on a dark navy-black ground; seed % 6 picks the variant and ⌊seed / 6⌋ the order of the panels; an exactly periodic pattern; a schematic picture, unlike `gameoflife` (one snapshot of an unsettled random soup); tests read the pattern of every panel back from the picture, check by the rules that each is a still life, that the joined variants are joined and that there are no repeats, compare the set with an enumeration that goes through all 2^(width times height) patterns of the box and tests each by the rules, and compare point by point the ink, height, tone and occlusion; the current catalog has 1158 kinds, 1159 gallery choices including plain.

- `alternatingsignmatrices`: Alternating sign matrices — every alternating sign matrix of one kind drawn once (a square of 0, 1 and −1 whose rows and columns add up to 1 and whose entries that are not 0 alternate in sign): all 7, 42 and 429 of orders 3, 4 and 5, the 18 of order 4 with a −1, the 67 of order 5 that are symmetric and the 200 of order 5 with exactly one −1; only the cells that are 1 or −1 are flat tiles (a −1 is darker and lower); lime on a dark olive-black ground; seed % 6 picks the variant and ⌊seed / 6⌋ the order of the panels; an exactly periodic pattern; a schematic picture, which partly overlaps `permutations` (24 of the 42 of order 4 are permutation matrices); tests read the matrix of every panel back from the picture, check by the definition that each is an alternating sign matrix of the kind and that there are no repeats, compare the set with an enumeration by monotone triangles, and compare point by point the ink, height, tone and occlusion; the current catalog has 1157 kinds, 1158 gallery choices including plain.

- `polysticks`: Polysticks — every polystick of one size (a connected shape of unit edges of the square lattice) drawn once: the free ones (turns and mirror images count once, each drawn with one of the 8 turns and flips) of 3, 4 and 5 sticks, 5, 16 and 55 of them, and the fixed ones (counted apart only by moving) of 3, 4 and 5 sticks, 22, 88 and 372 of them; a stick is a line with round ends, so joined sticks have no seam, and the tone and height of a shape follow its symmetry (1, 2, 4 or 8 of the turns and flips map it onto itself); teal on a dark teal-black ground; seed % 6 picks the variant and ⌊seed / 6⌋ the order of the panels and the turn of the free shapes; an exactly periodic pattern; a schematic picture, unlike `polyominoes` (square cells) and `selfavoidingwalks` (paths on lattice points); tests read the shape and symmetry level of every panel back from the picture, check that each has exactly n sticks, is connected and is not repeated, compare the set with an enumeration that goes through all the sets of n edges of a window and keeps the connected ones, and compare point by point the ink, height, tone and occlusion; the current catalog has 1156 kinds, 1157 gallery choices including plain.

- `polyhexes`: Polyhexes — every polyhex of one size (a shape of hexagonal cells joined by their edges) drawn once: the free ones (turns and mirror images count once, each drawn with one of the 12 turns and flips) of 4, 5 and 6 cells, 7, 22 and 82 of them, and the fixed ones (counted apart only by moving) of 3, 4 and 5 cells, 11, 44 and 186 of them; the cells of a shape join without a seam and its tone and height follow its symmetry (1, 2, 3, 4, 6 or 12 of the turns and flips map it onto itself); violet on a dark purple-black ground; seed % 6 picks the variant and ⌊seed / 6⌋ the order of the panels and the turn of the free shapes; an exactly periodic pattern; a schematic picture, unlike `polyominoes` (square cells); tests read the shape and symmetry level of every panel back from the picture, check that each has exactly n cells, is connected and is not repeated, compare the set with an enumeration that goes through all the sets of n cells of an n by n window and keeps the connected ones, and compare point by point the ink, height, tone and occlusion; the current catalog has 1155 kinds, 1156 gallery choices including plain.

- `compositions`: Integer compositions — every composition of one kind drawn once as a strip cut into pieces (the cells of a piece join, a gap parts the pieces): all the 128 and 256 compositions of 8 and 9 (2^(n − 1)), the 89 and 233 of 10 and 12 with no part above 2 (Fibonacci numbers), the 144 of 12 with only odd parts and the 101 with all parts different; the tone and height of a piece follow its length; blue-violet on a dark navy-black ground; seed % 6 picks the variant and ⌊seed / 6⌋ the order of the panels; an exactly periodic pattern; a schematic picture; tests read the composition of every panel back from the picture (cutting where the height at a seam is 0) and the height of every piece, check by the definitions that each is of the kind and that there are no repeats, compare the set with an enumeration that goes through all 2^(n − 1) ways to cut a strip and filters them, and compare point by point the ink, height, tone and occlusion; the current catalog has 1154 kinds, 1155 gallery choices including plain.

- `setpartitions`: Set partitions — every partition of a set into blocks of one kind drawn once as its restricted growth string (one lit tile in every column, its row the block of that element): all the 5, 15, 52 and 203 partitions of 3, 4, 5 and 6 elements (the Bell numbers) and the 42 and 132 noncrossing partitions of 5 and 6 elements (the Catalan numbers); the tone and height of a tile follow the size of its block; amber on a dark plum-black ground; seed % 6 picks the variant and ⌊seed / 6⌋ the order of the panels; an exactly periodic pattern; a schematic picture, unlike `youngdiagrams` (partitions of an integer) and `permutations` (permutation matrices); tests read the partition and height of every panel back from the picture, check by the definitions that each is of the kind and that there are no repeats, compare the set with an enumeration that goes through all n^n maps and renumbers and filters them, and compare point by point the ink, height, tone and occlusion; the current catalog has 1153 kinds, 1154 gallery choices including plain.

- `booleanfunctions`: Boolean functions — every Boolean function of one kind drawn once as its truth table (1 lit, 0 dim): all 16 of 2 variables, the 20 monotone, 16 self-dual and 256 in all of 3 variables, and the 168 monotone (the Dedekind number) and 256 self-dual of 4 variables; mint green on a dark green-black ground; seed % 6 picks the variant and ⌊seed / 6⌋ the order of the panels; an exactly periodic pattern; a schematic picture; tests read the truth table of every panel back from the picture, check by the definitions that each is of the kind and that there are no repeats, compare the set with an enumeration that goes through all 2^(2^n) tables and tests each, and compare point by point the ink, height, tone and occlusion; the current catalog has 1152 kinds, 1153 gallery choices including plain.

- `latinsquares`: Latin squares — every Latin square of one kind drawn once: all 12 of order 3, the 4 reduced, 48 diagonal and 576 in all of order 4, the 56 reduced of order 5 and the 288 Sudoku squares of order 4; every cell is a flat tile whose tone and height follow its symbol; rose pink on a dark plum-black ground; seed % 6 picks the variant and ⌊seed / 6⌋ the order of the panels; an exactly periodic pattern; a schematic picture, unlike `sudoku` (random digits), `graecolatin` (one square) and `cayleytable` (a group table); tests read the symbols of every panel back from the picture, check that each is a Latin square (and reduced, diagonal or Sudoku where the kind says so) and that there are no repeats, compare the set with another method (all permutations as rows, chosen row by row, restrictions applied last), check 576 = 4!·3!·4 and compare point by point the ink, height, tone and occlusion; the current catalog has 1151 kinds, 1152 gallery choices including plain.

- `dominoboards`: Domino boards — every domino tiling of a small board drawn once: the boards 6×2, 4×3, 4×4, 6×3, 5×4 and 6×4 give 13, 11, 36, 41, 95 and 281 tilings (Kasteleyn's formula); the two cells of a domino join without a seam and are parted from other cells by a gap, a horizontal domino brighter and higher than a vertical one; amber on a dark brown-black ground; seed % 6 picks the board and ⌊seed / 6⌋ the order of the panels; an exactly periodic pattern; a schematic picture, unlike `dominotiling` (which draws one random tiling of a torus); tests read the tiling of every panel back from the picture (the heights and the seams), check that every cell is joined to exactly one neighbour, that the joining is mutual and that there are no repeats, compare the set with an enumeration written the other way round and with Kasteleyn's formula, and compare point by point the ink, height, tone and occlusion (the gaps themselves too); the current catalog has 1150 kinds, 1151 gallery choices including plain.

- `rootedtrees`: Rooted trees — every rooted tree of n nodes drawn once up to renaming the nodes: the 9, 20, 48 and 115 trees of 5, 6, 7 and 8 nodes, and the 46 and 98 of 8 and 9 nodes with at most 2 children to a node; the root at the top and the children hanging from a bar; the tone and height of a dot follow its number of children; lime green on a dark green-black ground; seed % 6 picks the variant and ⌊seed / 6⌋ the order of the panels; an exactly periodic pattern; a schematic picture, unlike `spanningtrees` (all the spanning trees of one graph) and `graphatlas` (the isomorphism classes of small graphs); tests read the tree of every panel back from the picture (nodes, bars, children and numbers of children), check that its AHU-style name set equals the result of trying all lists of parents and that no two panels are alike, and compare point by point the ink, height, tone and occlusion; the current catalog has 1149 kinds, 1150 gallery choices including plain.

- `graphatlas`: Graph atlas — every graph of one kind drawn once up to renaming its vertices (one for each isomorphism class), with the vertices on a regular polygon and the edges as straight lines: all 11 graphs of 4 vertices, the 21 connected and all 34 graphs of 5 vertices, the 112 connected, all 156 and the 24 with exactly 7 edges of 6 vertices; the tone and height of a dot follow its degree; sky blue on a dark navy-black ground; seed % 6 picks the variant and ⌊seed / 6⌋ the order of the panels; an exactly periodic pattern; a schematic picture, unlike `spanningtrees` (all the spanning trees of one graph), `gabrielgraph` and `fareygraph`; tests read the edges and degrees of every panel back from the picture and compare the set of classes with another method (orbit marking to list the isomorphism classes, named by the least edge list over all renamings), check that no two panels are alike and that the number of all the graphs equals Pólya's count, and compare point by point the ink, height, tone and occlusion; the current catalog has 1148 kinds, 1149 gallery choices including plain.

- `standardtableaux`: Standard tableaux — every standard Young tableau of a shape (1 to n written into a Young diagram, increasing along every row and column) drawn once: the shapes (3,2,1), (5,3), (3,3,3), (4,3,1), (4,3,2) and (4,4,2) give 16, 28, 42, 70, 168 and 252 of them (the hook length formula); every cell is a flat tile whose tone and height follow its number (1 the darkest, n the brightest); mint green on a dark green-black ground; seed % 6 picks the variant and ⌊seed / 6⌋ the order of the panels; an exactly periodic pattern; a schematic picture, unlike `youngdiagrams` (which draws the partitions and hook lengths); tests read the tableau of every panel back from the picture and check the shape, the numbers, the increase along rows and columns and that there are no repeats or gaps (against a recursion written in the opposite direction in the test; both are recursions on the shape, so not entirely independent algorithms), that the counts equal the hook length formula and compare point by point the ink, height, tone and occlusion; the current catalog has 1147 kinds, 1148 gallery choices including plain.

- `polygondissections`: Polygon dissections — every way of cutting a regular polygon by diagonals that do not cross, drawn once: the triangulations of the hexagon, heptagon and octagon, 14, 42 and 132 of them (Catalan numbers), and the dissections of the pentagon, hexagon and heptagon, 11, 45 and 197 of them (little Schröder numbers); pieces are parted by gaps and the tone and height of a piece follow the fraction of its sides that are sides of the polygon; amber on a dark maroon-black ground; seed % 6 picks the variant and ⌊seed / 6⌋ the order of the panels; an exactly periodic pattern; a schematic picture, unlike `delaunay` (a triangulation of random points) and `dyckpaths` and `permutations` (the same Catalan numbers); tests read the set of diagonals of every panel back from the picture and compare it with the list of another method (all subsets by bit mask, crossing decided from the coordinates of the vertices), check that the counts equal the Kirkman–Cayley formula and that every triangulation has at least two ears, and compare point by point the ink, height, tone and occlusion; the current catalog has 1146 kinds, 1147 gallery choices including plain.

- `lozengetilings`: Lozenge tilings — every lozenge tiling of a hexagon with the sides a, b, c, a, b, c drawn once (the same as every plane partition in an a by b by c box, isometric stacks of cubes): the boxes [1,2,3], [2,2,2], [2,2,3], [2,2,4], [2,3,3] and [1,3,4] give 10, 20, 50, 105, 175 and 35 tilings (MacMahon's formula); three kinds of lozenge (top, right and left faces) in three tones; cyan-blue on a dark teal-black ground; seed % 6 picks the variant and ⌊seed / 6⌋ the order of the panels; an exactly periodic pattern; a schematic picture, unlike the fixed periodic `rhombille` and the Penrose family of rhombus tilings; tests read the three sets of lozenge centres of every panel back from the picture and compare them with another method (all the plane partitions by brute force, then the centres of the faces by the rule that a face between a cube and an empty place is seen; the sampler is a painter's algorithm), check that the numbers of the three kinds of lozenge are a b, b c and a c and that the total equals MacMahon's formula, and compare point by point the ink, height, tone and occlusion; the current catalog has 1145 kinds, 1146 gallery choices including plain.

- `permutations`: Permutations — every permutation of one kind drawn once as a permutation matrix: all 24 of 4 elements, all 120 of 5, the 132 of 6 that avoid 231 (a Catalan number), the 232 involutions of 7, the 265 derangements of 6 and the 120 cyclic permutations of 6; the tone and height of a permutation follow its inversions over the most there can be; the other cells of the box show a faint dot and the box ground is a little lighter and higher; lavender on a dark indigo-black ground; seed % 6 picks the variant and ⌊seed / 6⌋ the order of the panels; an exactly periodic pattern; a schematic picture, unlike `cayleytable` and `graecolatin`; tests read the permutation of every panel back from the picture and compare the set with one listed by another method (all permutations by the next-permutation rule, filtered and checked by a stack simulation, cycle decomposition and counting bubble sort swaps), check that the inversions of the 5-element permutations follow the Mahonian numbers and compare point by point the ink, height, tone and occlusion; the current catalog has 1144 kinds, 1145 gallery choices including plain.

- `selfavoidingwalks`: Self-avoiding walks — every self-avoiding walk on the square lattice (never visiting a point twice) drawn once, always starting to the right: all the walks of 4, 5 and 6 steps, 25, 71 and 195 of them, and the walks of 5, 6 and 7 steps with mirror images left out, 36, 98 and 272 of them; round-cornered thick lines over a faint grid of dots with the start a big bright dot; the tone and height of a walk follow the squared distance between its start and its end (a straight walk is the highest and brightest); pink on a dark violet-black ground; seed % 6 picks the variant and ⌊seed / 6⌋ the order of the panels; an exactly periodic pattern; a schematic picture, unlike `dyckpaths` (Dyck and Motzkin paths) and `aggregationfrost` (aggregation of random walkers); tests read the walk of every panel back from the picture (following its edges) and check that each has exactly n edges, no branching and no repeated vertex, that the set equals a recursion written again in the test (the same kind of recursion rewritten, not another algorithm), that the count with the mirror images left out is (full − 1) / 2 + 1, and compare point by point the ink, height, tone and occlusion; the current catalog has 1143 kinds, 1144 gallery choices including plain.

- `polyominoes`: Polyominoes — every polyomino of n cells drawn once: free ones (a shape and its turns and flips count once) of 4, 6 and 7 cells, 5, 35 and 108 of them, and fixed ones (only moving counts as the same) of 4, 5 and 6 cells, 19, 63 and 216 of them; the cells of a shape join without a seam and are parted from other cells by a gap; the tone and height of a shape follow the number of its symmetries (1, 2, 4 or 8); peach on a dark navy-black ground; seed % 6 picks the variant and ⌊seed / 6⌋ the order of the panels (and the turns of free ones); an exactly periodic pattern; a schematic picture, unlike `pentominotiles` (a fixed layout of the 12 pentominoes), `trominotiling`, `dominotiling` and `youngdiagrams`; tests read the shape of every panel back from the picture and check that each has exactly n cells, is connected and that there are no repeats or gaps (against a grow-by-one-cell list written again in the test, the same algorithm rewritten and not another one), read the symmetry level back and compare point by point the ink, height, tone and occlusion; the current catalog has 1142 kinds, 1143 gallery choices including plain.

- `spanningtrees`: Spanning trees — every spanning tree of a small graph (K4, K(2,4), the wheel W4, K(3,3), the wheel W5 and K5) drawn once: tree edges thick and bright, other edges thin and dim and vertices as dots; 16, 32, 45, 81, 121 and 125 spanning trees (Cayley's formula and the matrix tree theorem); mint green on a dark teal-black ground; seed % 6 picks the graph and ⌊seed / 6⌋ the order of the panels; an exactly periodic pattern; a schematic picture, related to but unlike `euclideanmst` and `hamiltoncycle` (which draw one random tree); tests read the edge set of every panel back from the picture and check that each is a spanning tree and that there are no repeats or gaps (the same as an enumeration of all choices of edges and as the count by Kirchhoff's determinant), that groups of seeds change only the order, and compare point by point the ink, height, tone and occlusion of the edges and vertices; the current catalog has 1141 kinds, 1142 gallery choices including plain.
