- `confocalconics`: Confocal conic grid — elliptic coordinates about two foci: the ellipses d1 + d2 = 2f·cosh μ and the hyperbolas d1 − d2 = 2f·cos ν cut at right angles, the grid lines at equal steps of μ and ν, the cells curved squares (conformal) coloured by the parity of the sum of their two indices (cells across the segment between the foci or the axis outside them have opposite colours), thin rose-pink lines on dark purple-red; the seed picks one of 6 sets (f, m, angle); unlike `conformalspiral` (complex logarithm, no foci) and `ellipsebilliard` (chords in an ellipse); drawn pixel by pixel from the elliptic coordinates; the tests read only the drawing back and, with a forward construction from the standard parametrisation, check that the lines are bright and that other sets have check points reading lower, with the focal property (d1 + d2 and d1 − d2 constant) that bright line pixels lie on an ellipse or hyperbola (the tolerance is wide and a mutation check with a small shift did not trip it) and that the cell middles are two-coloured by the parity of i + j; they do not cover the fade zone or the radii below 0.12 and above 0.85; the set for each seed was measured by a probe and written into the test, not an independent oracle; an authored schematic with no specific source cited and no photograph compared. Defaults: 0.5 m tile, 0.0008 UV relief (not metres), metallic 0; sampling time was not measured. Current catalog: 1084 kinds, 1085 gallery choices including plain.

- `steinerchain`: Steiner chain — between two circles, one inside the other and not concentric, a ring of n circles each touching its two neighbours, the inner circle and the outer circle and closing up (the Steiner porism), thin pale-blue lines on dark blue, the beads filled in order with two alternating tones; the seed picks one of 6 pairs (n, shift) ((6, 0) concentric, (8, 0.35), (10, 0.5), (12, 0.45), (6, 0.55), (8, 0.2)) and a random turn; the eccentric chain comes from the concentric one by a Mobius map; unlike `apollonianmosaic` (nineteen tangent circles from Descartes reflection) this is a ring of circles between two eccentric circles, the number changing with the variant; the tests read only the drawing back and do not use the construction: the connected regions of the reading are taken as the beads and they check the number of beads, the roundness, that the insets from the three kinds of tangency agree everywhere, that the inner circle agrees with the formula of the Mobius map and that the tones alternate; the variant for each seed was measured by a probe and written into the test, not an independent oracle; sets 0 and 1 were not viewed in previews; an authored schematic with no specific source cited and no photograph compared. Defaults: 0.5 m tile, 0.0008 UV relief (not metres), metallic 0; sampling time was not measured. Current catalog: 1083 kinds, 1084 gallery choices including plain.

- `conformalspiral`: Conformal spiral checkerboard — a lattice of equal squares in the complex logarithm (side vectors A = (a, b) and B = (−b, a), a turn being exactly p times A plus q times B), which on the disc is curved squares shrinking without end towards the middle (conformal, all angles right angles, self-similar, two-coloured by the parity of the sum i + j of the two cell numbers), thin amber lines on dark purple with the line width fixed in the logarithm plane; the seed picks one of 6 pairs (p, q) ((15, 1), (17, 3), (19, 5), (11, 11), (17, −7), (21, −1)), q = 0 being a log-polar grid; it is not the full Droste effect (no picture-within-picture); unlike `sacksspiral`, `vogelspiral` and `ulamspiral` (points or disks along a spiral) it is a checkerboard of the whole plane; drawn pixel by pixel by taking the complex logarithm; the tests read only the drawing back and with a forward construction from the cells to the picture check that the cell sides are bright, that other sets have check points reading lower and that the middles of the cells are two-coloured by the parity of i + j; this is a self-consistency check (both sides share the lattice definition, so it does not show the definition itself is right) and does not cover the fade zones and the edge of the view; the set for each seed was measured by a probe and written into the test, not an independent oracle; sets 2 and 3 were not viewed in previews; an authored schematic with no specific source cited and no photograph compared. Defaults: 0.5 m tile, 0.0008 UV relief (not metres), metallic 0; sampling time was not measured. Current catalog: 1082 kinds, 1083 gallery choices including plain.

- `fareydisc`: Farey disc — the Poincare disc tiled by ideal triangles (all corners on the circle), the Farey tessellation, as thin green lines on dark teal, neighbouring triangles coloured dark and bright by the parity of the number of mirrors between them and the central triangle (neighbours always differ), the line width fixed in hyperbolic terms and a ring at the circle, the seed picking one of 5 viewpoints (the centre of the central triangle, the middle of an edge and 3 shifted positions); unlike `fareygraph` (the upper half-plane, only lines) and `poincaretiling` and `poincaretriangles` (finite corners) the set of edges is that of the Farey graph but drawn in the disc, with all corners at infinity, the parity two-colouring and 5 viewpoints; drawn pixel by pixel by folding with inversions; the tests read only the drawing back and, with a completely different construction (the triangles of the Stern–Brocot tree, the Cayley map and a Mobius map from the cross ratio), verify that all the check points on the edges of the viewpoint are bright, that the other viewpoints have check points reading lower, and that the colour at the centres of the triangles is a two-colouring by the parity of the depth in the tree; they do not cover the edge of the view and there is no check that places far from the edges are not lines (the list of edges is incomplete); the viewpoint for each seed was measured by a probe and written into the test, not an independent oracle; the C1 threshold was passed by tuning the viewpoint shifts and the fade start (stated); an authored schematic with no specific source cited and no photograph compared. Defaults: 0.5 m tile, 0.0008 UV relief (not metres), metallic 0; sampling time was not measured. Current catalog: 1081 kinds, 1082 gallery choices including plain.

- `sphericaltriangles`: Stereographic view of mirror triangles — all the mirror great circles of a polyhedral symmetry group (15 for the icosahedral group, 9 for the cube group, 6 for the tetrahedral group) as seen by stereographic projection (thin amber lines on dark brown, the line width fixed on the sphere), the spherical triangles they cut out coloured dark and bright by the parity of the number of mirrors (neighbours always differ), the seed picking one of 6 sets (the icosahedral group along 5-, 3- and 2-fold axes, the cube group along 4- and 3-fold axes and the tetrahedral group along a 3-fold axis); unlike `sphericaltiling` (only edges and mirror lines) it has the parity two-colouring and other viewing axes; drawn pixel by pixel by folding with mirrors; the tests read only the drawing back and, with a completely different construction (the mirror normals written as closed forms), verify that the mirror circles of the set are bright everywhere, that other sets have check points reading lower, that places far from the lines are not bright lines and that the two-colouring is the product of the signs of `n·P` over all mirrors; they do not cover the edge of the view; the projection formula and the viewing-axis convention are shared by both sides, and the set for each seed was measured by a probe and written into the test, not an independent oracle; sets 1 and 3 were not viewed in previews; an authored schematic with no specific source cited and no photograph compared. Defaults: 0.5 m tile, 0.0008 UV relief (not metres), metallic 0; sampling time was not measured. Current catalog: 1080 kinds, 1081 gallery choices including plain.

- `poincaretriangles`: Poincaré-disc triangle group — the triangle group (2, p, q) of the hyperbolic plane: all the mirror lines of the regular tiling {p, q} drawn as thin rose-pink lines, the triangles they cut out coloured dark and bright by the parity of the number of mirrors (neighbouring triangles always differ), the line width fixed in the hyperbolic metric, the seed picking one of (3,7), (3,8), (4,5), (4,6), (5,5) and (3,9); unlike `poincaretiling` (only the edges and polygons of the tiling); drawn pixel by pixel by folding with mirrors; the tests read only the drawing back and, with a completely different construction (mirroring triangles in their sides again and again, and taking points on geodesics with a Möbius map), verify that 2p, 2q and 4 triangles meet at O, B and A, that exactly one set fits, that the lines are bright everywhere, that places far from them are not bright lines and the two-colouring by parity; they do not cover the rim of the disc (radius > 0.9); the set for each seed was measured by a probe and written into the test, not an independent oracle; the previews do not cover all the sets; an authored schematic with no specific source cited and no photograph compared. Defaults: 0.5 m tile, 0.0008 UV relief (not metres), metallic 0; sampling time was not measured. Current catalog: 1079 kinds, 1080 gallery choices including plain.

- `sphericaltiling`: Stereographic view of a regular polyhedron — the edges of a regular polyhedron as seen by stereographic projection (thin cyan lines on dark blue-black, each face brighter from its centre to its edge, the line width fixed on the sphere), the seed picking one of the edges of the icosahedron, the dodecahedron, the cube, the octahedron and the tetrahedron and the 15 mirror planes of the icosahedral group; drawn pixel by pixel by folding with the mirror group; the tests read only the drawing back and build the lines in a completely different way (the corners from the usual coordinates, the pairs of nearest corners as edges, points by spherical interpolation), verifying that the lines of the set are bright everywhere, that other sets have check points reading lower and that places far from the lines are not bright lines; they do not cover the edge of the view; the projection formula and the viewing-axis convention are shared by both sides, and the set for each seed was measured by a probe and written into the test, not an independent oracle; the cube and mirrors sets were not viewed in previews; an authored schematic with no specific source cited and no photograph compared. Defaults: 0.5 m tile, 0.0008 UV relief (not metres), metallic 0; sampling time was not measured. Current catalog: 1078 kinds, 1079 gallery choices including plain.

- `poincaretiling`: Poincaré-disc hyperbolic tiling — the regular tiling {p, q} of the hyperbolic plane drawn in the Poincaré disc (thin gold lines on dark blue, each polygon brighter from its centre to its edge, the line width fixed in the hyperbolic metric so it gets thinner towards the circle), the seed picking one of {7,3}, {5,4}, {4,5}, {6,4}, {8,3} and {3,7}; drawn by folding with mirrors; the tests read only the drawing back and, with a completely different construction (mirroring polygons in their sides again and again, and taking points on geodesics with a Möbius map), verify that exactly q polygons meet at a corner, that exactly one set fits, that the lines are bright everywhere and that places far from them are not bright lines; they do not cover the rim of the disc (radius > 0.9); the set for each seed was measured by a probe and written into the test, not an independent oracle; only 3 of the 6 sets were viewed in previews; an authored schematic with no specific source cited and no photograph compared. Defaults: 0.5 m tile, 0.0008 UV relief (not metres), metallic 0; sampling time was not measured. Current catalog: 1077 kinds, 1078 gallery choices including plain.

- `pursuitpolygons`: Pursuit-polygon swirl — n bugs on the corners of a regular n-gon each run at the next one; after each step every corner moves the fraction f of the way to the next, and the sides of K + 1 smaller, turned polygons are drawn (pale-pink thin lines on dark purple-black, the line getting thinner from the outside in, n = 4 looking like rotating squares), the seed picking one of 6 sets (number of corners, f, K), with all of the figure inside the tile; the tests read only the drawing back and compute all the sides independently from a closed form (every step multiplies the whole polygon by the complex number `1 − f + f·ω`), verifying that exactly one set fits, that the sides are bright everywhere and that places far from them are dark; the set for each seed was measured by a probe and written into the test, not an independent oracle; only 2 of the 6 sets were viewed in previews; an authored schematic with no specific source cited and no photograph compared. Defaults: 0.5 m tile, 0.0008 UV relief (not metres), metallic 0; construction and sampling times were not measured. Current catalog: 1076 kinds, 1077 gallery choices including plain.

- `lsystemplants`: L-system plants — branching plants grown by Lindenmayer systems (parallel string rewriting) and drawn by a turtle as pale-green thin lines (dark green-black ground, the plant scaled to fill 0.84 of the tile and growing upward), the seed picking one of 6 sets (looking like a fractal plant, a bush, a tree, a weed, a seaweed and a ternary tree; the rules are from my memory and may differ from the common versions), with all of the figure inside the tile; unlike `pythagorastree`, `vicsekfractal` and `dragoncurve`; the tests read only the drawing back and recompute the segments independently in another way (recursion on letters, a rotation matrix for turning), verifying that exactly one set fits, that the lines are bright everywhere and that places far from them are dark; the set for each seed was measured by a probe and written into the test, not an independent oracle; only 3 of the 6 sets were viewed in previews; an authored schematic with no specific source cited and no photograph compared. Defaults: 0.5 m tile, 0.0008 UV relief (not metres), metallic 0; construction and sampling times were not measured. Current catalog: 1075 kinds, 1076 gallery choices including plain.

- `ellipsebilliard`: Elliptical billiard path — a ball goes straight inside an elliptical table and is thrown back by the law of reflection at the wall; the 110 to 160 chords are drawn (thin mint lines on dark blue-black, with a thicker elliptical wall), leaving a region without lines bounded by their envelope (from memory I believe a caustic, not verified), the seed picking one of 6 sets and all of the figure staying inside the tile; unlike `stringart` (times-table chords on a circle); the tests read only the drawing back and recompute the path independently in another way (a quadratic for the hit, reflection about the tangent), verifying that exactly one set fits, that the chords and the wall are bright everywhere and that places far from them are dark; the set for each seed was measured by a probe and written into the test, not an independent oracle; an authored schematic with no specific source cited and no photograph compared. Defaults: 0.5 m tile, 0.0008 UV relief (not metres), metallic 0; construction and sampling times were not measured. Current catalog: 1074 kinds, 1075 gallery choices including plain.

- `lorenzattractor`: Lorenz attractor path — the Lorenz system (σ = 10, ρ = 28, β = 8/3) integrated from one of 6 start points with a fourth-order Runge-Kutta method for 8 time units, projected on the x–z, y–z or x–y plane and drawn as one thin amber line (dark purple-black ground, the ink getting thinner), one per tile at a fixed position so it does not cross the seam, the seed picking the start set; unlike `harmonograph` (an artificial figure of damped waves) it is a real trajectory of a chaotic equation; the tests read only the drawing back and verify with an independent integration at a quarter of the step that exactly one start set fits, that the path is bright everywhere and that places far from it are dark (chaos is why the time is only 8; my first choice of 22 did not work); the start set for each seed was measured by a probe and written into the test, not an independent oracle; the thinning of the ink is not tested separately; an authored schematic with no specific source cited and no photograph compared. Defaults: 0.5 m tile, 0.0008 UV relief (not metres), metallic 0; construction and sampling times were not measured. Current catalog: 1073 kinds, 1074 gallery choices including plain.

- `harmonograph`: Harmonograph figure — the figure of a two-pendulum harmonograph, one large figure per tile (x and y each the sum of two damped sine waves, t from 0 to 60, a thin pale-blue line on dark blue-black, the loops winding in to nested spirals and the ink growing thinner), the seed picking one of 6 parameter sets, with all of the figure inside the tile; unlike `lissajoustable` (a 6×6 table of small undamped curves), `spirographtable` and `rhodoneatable`; the tests read only the drawing back and verify, with independently computed curve points, that exactly one parameter set fits, that the curve is bright everywhere and that places far from it are dark; the parameter set for each seed was measured by a probe and written into the test, not an independent oracle; the thinning of the ink is not tested separately; an authored schematic with no specific source cited and no photograph compared. Defaults: 0.5 m tile, 0.0008 UV relief (not metres), metallic 0; construction and sampling times were not measured. Current catalog: 1072 kinds, 1073 gallery choices including plain.

- `delaunay`: Delaunay triangulation low-poly pattern — the Delaunay triangulation of a Poisson-disk point set on the torus (minimum distance 0.085), each triangle a flat copper fill (tone 0.25 to 0.6, random), thin dark-navy seams between the triangles and a small dot at each point, with exactly 3 times as many edges as points and 2 times as many triangles as points; unlike `gabrielgraph` (Gabriel edges and points only) and `euclideanmst` (the minimum spanning tree) it is the whole triangulation, filled; the tests read only the drawing back and judge the edges (pairs whose margin is close to 0 are not judged), the face count and the fills independently from the empty-circumcircle definition; they do not prove the algorithm for arbitrary point sets; an authored schematic with no specific source cited and no photograph compared. Defaults: 0.5 m tile, 0.0008 UV relief (not metres), metallic 0; construction and sampling times were not measured. Current catalog: 1071 kinds, 1072 gallery choices including plain.

- `perfectarray`: m-sequence perfect array — the maximal length sequence (1023 bits) of a 10-bit LFSR folded by the Chinese remainder theorem into a 31×33 binary array (512 bright cells) whose torus autocorrelation is exactly −1 at all 1,022 non-zero shifts, the seed picking the mask and starting state; the tests do not reproduce the LFSR but read the drawing back and verify the autocorrelation from its definition (six seeds, with a negative control), a probe checking 200 more seeds; no general theorem is proved and only 31×33 was done; an authored schematic with no specific source cited and no photograph compared. Defaults: 0.5 m tile, 0.0008 UV relief (not metres), metallic 0; construction and sampling times were not measured. Current catalog: 1070 kinds, 1071 gallery choices including plain.

- `kaprekar`: Kaprekar constant table — Kaprekar's routine on 4-digit numbers (arrange the digits into the biggest and smallest number and subtract): a 100×100 table whose cell (row, column) shows how many subtractions the number 100·row + column needs to reach 6174 (9 teal tones, 0 to 7 subtractions, the 10 repdigits darkest), the seed moving it around the torus; the tests recompute all 10,000 numbers in a different way (taking the digits by division) and confirm the counts and that exactly one shift fits; they do not prove the theorem itself and only 4 digits were done; low contrast, which I do not think is especially attractive; an authored schematic with no specific source cited and no photograph compared. Defaults: 0.5 m tile, 0.0008 UV relief (not metres), metallic 0; construction and sampling times were not measured. Current catalog: 1069 kinds, 1070 gallery choices including plain.

- `hypercube`: Hypercube projection — the plane projection of a hypercube of 4 to 7 dimensions (picked by the seed): vertices are bit strings joined when they differ in one bit, bit k moving a vertex by ±½ along the unit vector at the angle k·π/n (the symmetric Petrie-polygon projection), brighter with more bits set, a thin pink network; the tests check only through sampler read-backs, against a projection geometry written separately in the test, that all edges are lit and that the other chords agree with their distance from the drawing (margins of 0.002 and 0.004, 1,428 chords of the 7-dimensional picture not judged); they do not show that the angle choice is the standard way to draw it or that every seed falls on the four dimensions; an authored schematic with no specific source cited and no photograph compared. Defaults: 0.5 m tile, 0.0008 UV relief (not metres), metallic 0; construction and sampling times were not measured. Current catalog: 1068 kinds, 1069 gallery choices including plain.

- `fareygraph`: Farey graph — the Farey graph of an order from 7 to 12 (picked by the seed), the vertices being the reduced fractions with a denominator up to the order and a / b < c / d joined when c b − a d = 1, each edge drawn as a semicircular arc and mirrored in the baseline, thin cyan arcs on deep blue; the tests check only through sampler read-backs that the arcs drawn agree exactly with the Farey neighbour criterion, that there are 35/43/55/63/83/91 arcs and that no arcs of the next order are drawn; they do not show that every point along an arc is on a circle or that every seed falls on the six orders; the first version had a crease at the tile edges, which the C1 test caught and which was fixed; an authored schematic with no specific source cited and no photograph compared. (Different from the existing `fordcircles`, which draws Ford discs.) Defaults: 0.5 m tile, 0.0008 UV relief (not metres), metallic 0; construction and sampling times were not measured. Current catalog: 1067 kinds, 1068 gallery choices including plain.

- `knightstour`: Knight's tour — a knight's tour of a 16×16 board by Warnsdorff's rule (up to 60 retries, ties decided by the hash), the squares as dots and the knight's moves as segments with a tone that brightens from 0.45 of the span at the start to the whole span at the end, an amber web; the tests check only through sampler read-backs, using 13 sample points to decide whether two squares a knight's move apart are joined, that all 256 squares are lit, that there are two ends with all others at degree 2 and that walking along the joins visits exactly 256 squares; this is not shown for every seed (only eight test seeds and 100 probe seeds were checked), the rule was written from memory without looking it up, and a first read-back with only 5 sample points misjudged some pairs and was corrected; an authored schematic with no specific source cited and no photograph compared. Defaults: 0.5 m tile, 0.0008 UV relief (not metres), metallic 0; construction and sampling times were not measured. Current catalog: 1066 kinds, 1067 gallery choices including plain.

- `daynight`: Day and Night — a 32×32 torus where a dead cell is born with 3, 6, 7 or 8 live neighbours and a live cell stays with 3, 4, 6, 7 or 8, a random soup of 50% density warmed up for 24 generations, the tile showing four consecutive generations (2×2 panels of 32×32 cells), a pale yellow and dark blue-grey camouflage; the tests check only through sampler read-backs that each of the last three panels is exactly the next generation of the panel before it by this rule, with the ordinary Life rule as a control; they do not show that the rule's name is right (from memory), that the soup is uniformly random or that the warm-up is right; an authored schematic with no specific source cited and no photograph compared. (Different from the existing `gameoflife` and `elementaryautomata`.) Defaults: 0.5 m tile, 0.0008 UV relief (not metres), metallic 0; construction and sampling times were not measured. - `hamiltoncycle`: Hamiltonian cycle — a random Hamiltonian cycle of a 32×32 grid (a closed path through every cell exactly once): a random spanning tree of a 16×16 coarse grid is thickened, and a thick lime-green line winds like a labyrinth; the tests check only through sampler read-backs that all 1,024 cell centres are lit, that every cell is joined to exactly two neighbours and that walking along the joins visits exactly 1,024 cells and returns to the start; this is not shown for every seed (only the six test seeds and 60 probe seeds were checked) and the construction was written from memory without looking it up; an authored schematic with no specific source cited and no photograph compared. (The closest existing kinds are `moorecurve` and `perfectmaze`; this one is a random 32×32 Hamiltonian cycle.) - `paleyhadamard`: Paley Hadamard — a Hadamard matrix from Paley's first construction, a prime q (43, 47, 59, 67, 71 or 79, picked by the seed) giving order q + 1, with row 0 all +1, column 0 below it all −1 and the body the quadratic character of (i − j), +1 bright gold and −1 dark blue-grey, with diagonal bands and a cross along row 0 and column 0; the tests check only through sampler read-backs, pair by pair from the definition, that any two rows are orthogonal, and that flipping an entry or reading at a wrong order breaks the orthogonality; they do not show the theorem behind Paley's construction or that every seed falls on one of the six orders; an authored schematic with no specific source cited and no photograph compared. - `cayleytable`: Cayley table — the multiplication table of a group of order 16, the seed picking one of Z16, Z8×Z2, Z4×Z4, Z4×Z2×Z2, Z2⁴ and the dihedral group of the octagon, 16×16 rose-toned tiles with element 0 as the identity; the tests check only through sampler read-backs that it is a Latin square with the identity and associative for all 4,096 triples, and identify the six groups by whether the table is symmetric, the number of elements squaring to the identity and the largest order; the expected invariants were worked out by hand and not checked against the literature, and the tables are not shown to be cell for cell the textbook ones; an authored schematic with no specific source cited and no photograph compared. - `schelling`: Schelling segregation — a 48×48 torus with about 10% empty cells and two kinds of resident of about 45% each; a resident is happy when at least 40% of its 8 neighbours that are occupied are of its own kind, and unhappy residents move in turn to the first empty cell where they would be happy (at most 60 sweeps), forming amber and brown patches like camouflage with dark plum empty cells; the tests check only through sampler read-backs, with a happiness test written separately in the test from the definition, that every resident is happy and none could be made happy by an empty cell; they do not show that the state came from one particular order of moves or that every seed converges; an authored schematic with no specific source cited and no photograph compared. Defaults: 0.08 m tile, 0.0042 UV relief (not metres), metallic1; explicit0 changes the factor, not the mask. Prefer 512; 32 is a coarse preview. Current catalog: 886 kinds, 887 gallery choices including plain.

- `rcafemalefaces`: RCA/Cinch female-face schematics — four broad round black washers, smaller round metal shoulders, still smaller six-flat front barrels, dark insulators and recessed socket annuli over opaque dark floors. No BNC bayonet lugs or large rear nuts on the front. Neutral gray omits real gold optics; shapes, relief and color are authored approximations, a connector-family variation, not electrical/audio/RF/fit/EMC/safety certification. The catalog then had 879 kinds.
- `rj45femalefaces`: RJ45-type 8P8C female-face schematics — four thin rectangular metal shields, matte-black insulating liners and low main wells, with narrower stepped latch extensions on one side and single rows of eight isolated long metal fingers opposite. No LEDs; neutral gray omits gold-plating optics, and the authored layout is not exact CAD or Ethernet/PoE/Cat/speed/electrical/EMC simulation or certification.
- `usbcfemalefaces`: USB-C female-face schematics — four rounded-flat metal mouths with recessed matte-dark wells, thin central insulating tongues and two rows of 12 metal pads per face (24 total), distinct from the existing four-contact rectangular sockets. Neutral-gray approximation, not gold-plating optics, cavity geometry, electrical/USB speed/PD simulation or safety certification.
- `iecc6inlets`: IEC C6 male inlet-face schematics — four flat black-PA6 flanges with shared cloverleaf wells, upper-single/lower-pair round metal tips and two countersunk mounts; tips stay below the flange without a C8 guard, not C5 female sockets, C14 blades or mains/safety simulation.
- `iecc8inlets`: IEC C8 male inlet-face schematics — four black-PA6 flanges with capsule guards and rounded-V waists around shared figure-eight wells; two round metal tips rise above each floor but remain below the guard, not C7 female holes, C14 blades or mains/safety simulation.
- `din5femalefaces`: standard five-pin DIN female-face schematics — four nonmetal black-PBT flanges with three mounts, raised circular mouths, keyed upper cutouts/lower webs and five centrally bulged elongated female mouths; opaque interior stand-ins, not mini-DIN, geometric holes or MIDI/electrical simulation.
- `xlrfemalefaces`: XLR female mating-face schematics — four rounded rectangular D-size metal flanges, two diagonal mounting holes, raised circular mouths, black inserts, top latches and open inner collars; upper-pair/lower-center recessed mouths have opaque stand-in floors, not male pins, geometric holes or audio/electrical simulation.
- `bncfemalefaces`: BNC female mating-face schematics — four low metal flanges, raised barrel rims, opposed paired studs and white dielectric annuli with simplified metal socket lips and opaque low floors; neutral-gray PBR, not male pins, geometric bores or RF simulation.
- `iecc14inlets`: IEC C14 male inlet-face schematics — four black keyed shrouds each enclose one upper and two lower solid metal blade tips, raised from low floors but below protective rims; not C13 female sockets, perforated rear tabs or mains simulation.
- `dsubsocketfaces`: DE-9 female mating-face schematics — four metal flanges, closed raised D-frames, black inserts and nine recessed sockets in five/four rows; not protruding gold pins, rear solder cups, transparent geometric holes or connectable circuitry.
- `topadjusttrimmers`: top-adjust trimmer-face schematics — four dielectric rectangular housings with small corner-offset round metal screws and single full-width straight blind slots; floors remain metal, not through-holes, large central fasteners or adjustable circuit simulation.
- `crownprobetips`: four-point crown probe-end schematics — nine round metal faces each have four rounded raised contacts, valleys and a lower metal cup; not cross-driver recesses, gears, castellated nuts, through-holes or spring/electrical simulation.
- `piezodiaphragms`: piezo diaphragm face schematics — four flat metal plates with ivory ceramic margins and single central metallic electrodes; not ceramic holes, speaker cones, lenses, split feedback electrodes or circuit/vibration/audio simulation.
- `castellatedboards`: PCB edge-half-hole faces — four dark-green dielectric boards each carry four banks of seven edge-open notches and pale metallic crescent lands; not closed round vias, QFN contacts, castellated nuts, geometric holes or PCB fabrication/electrical/thermal certification.
- `qfnlandpads`: QFN underside flat lands — four dark dielectric packages each carry four banks of seven silver flat contacts and a separate central exposed pad; not BGA domes, PCB thermal vias, pinouts/always-ground pads or dimensional/thermal/electrical certification.
- `radiolariandiscs`: radiolarian equatorial cutaways — four ivory concentric-ring skeletons each enclose a central chamber, 6/12/18-cell annular rows and six short marginal spines; opaque dielectric, not external sieve plates, spiderwebs, geometric holes or species/microscopy calibration.
- `bgasolderballs`: complete BGA underside matrices — four dark square dielectric carriers each hold a uniform 13 × 13 field of silver metallic rounded domes and contact shadows; not bearing races, ball chains, pinouts, JEDEC dimensions, solder-alloy or reflow certification.
- `pigskinleather`: schematic pigskin grain — 64 irregular triplets of separate rounded dark recesses in warm-brown matte leather; opaque dielectric, not individual triangular holes, raised ostrich collars, uniform punched perforations or material authentication.
- `trianglesocketheads`: TA triangular blind-socket round heads — 25 silver-gray steel heads retain three straight drive sides and closed flat metallic floors above rough dielectric gray backing; not Y drives, curved TP3, geometric holes or dimensional/security certification.
- `diatomvalves`: schematic naviculoid external valves — four ivory lanceolate faces with lineolate striae, a flush central clear area and two centrally separated raphes with same-sided terminal bends; opaque dielectric, not geometric pores, complete paired cells or calibrated microscopy.
- `chitonshells`: schematic chiton dorsal armor — nine oval groups each retain eight raised valves, seven directional lap joints and a rough dark mantle girdle; warm-brown opaque dielectric, not articulated shell meshes, rolling motion or biomechanics.
- `leafstomata`: schematic leaf stomata — 25 kidney-shaped guard-cell pairs enclose dark recessed pores, retaining lower raised polar contacts; an opaque green dielectric pattern, not geometric air passages, a complete epidermal reconstruction or live transpiration model.
- `watermelontourmaline`: watermelon tourmaline slice mosaic — 16 rounded-triangular pink cores/pale-gray bands/green rinds share a polished plane above rough gray backing; a front-lit opaque palette approximation, without transmission, refraction, pleochroism or gem certification.
- `cuttlebone`: schematic cuttlebone wall–septa section — uneven layers and staggered, oppositely bending thin supports over dark recessed backing; warm-white rough dielectric and fully opaque, not a 3D labyrinth reconstruction, geometric holes or biomechanics model.
- `pallasite`: polished pallasite — nearly coplanar olive/honey dielectric crystals and a connected silver-gray iron-nickel matrix with fine dark intramineral fissures; an opaque front-lit PBR approximation, without backlit transmission, calibrated alloy optics or meteorite certification.
- `gosperinlay`: order-three Gosper brass inlay — 343 equal metallic links with just two round endpoints and no branches over rough navy dielectric backing; a finite open curve, not a closed Moore groove, limiting space-filling set or physical inlay mesh.
- `vogelspiral`: golden-angle disk mosaic — 97 equal flat teal/sage/ivory disks on rough gray backing, a discrete Vogel pattern with an authored half-step radial offset; not a continuous spiral, tangent Doyle packing or plant simulation.
- `langtonsant`: 1,024-step Langton-ant snapshot tiles — 122 glossy dark cells on a pale-warm 32 × 32 board, with a raised cream compass triangle; a fixed white-left/black-right authored snapshot, not a QR code or live simulation.
- `pentominotiles`: twelve free pentomino glazed tiles — five cells per piece, all twelve shapes once per 6 × 10 block, 180 navy/slate/ivory pieces above rough grout with no internal grid seams. An authored fixed packing, not an interactive puzzle or regular-pentagon tiling.
- `pythagorastree`: finite glazed-square Pythagoras trees — four branching motifs, each 31 red-brown-to-ivory squares in five size generations; an opaque dielectric grout-backed design, not two-size plane tiling, an infinite fractal or an actual tree.
- `ichinghexagrams`: sixty-four raised hexagram figures — six solid/broken lines each, 576 flat rounded-shoulder dark bars on rough cream backing; authored binary order, not King Wen numbering, divination or a font.
- `apollonianmosaic`: Apollonian tangent-circle mosaic — teal, sage and ivory glazed disks, nineteen unequal tangent circles per motif after two Descartes-reflection rounds; a finite opaque dielectric authored design over rough grout, not an infinite fractal or installation specification.
- `prismaticpentagonaltiles`: prismatic pentagonal (V3.3.3.4.4) tiling — alternating up/down blue-gray, sage and ivory glazed houses, two adjacent 90° base corners and three 120° roof corners; authored opaque dielectric tiles in rough grout, without dimensional or installation certification.
- `floretpentagonaltiles`: chiral floret pentagonal (V3.3.3.3.6) tiling — forest/olive/ivory glazed pentagons, six petals per flower, each with one 60° and four 120° corners; authored opaque dielectric tiles in rough grout, without dimensional or installation certification.
- `snubtrihexagonaltiles`: chiral snub trihexagonal (3.3.3.3.6) tiling — indigo hexagons and ivory/tan triangles, four triangles and one hexagon at each vertex; an authored opaque dielectric glazed-tile approximation over rough grout, without real dimensions or installation certification.
- `deltoidaltrihexagonaltiles`: deltoidal trihexagonal (V3.4.6.4) tiling — kite-shaped burgundy, rose and ivory glazed tiles over rough gray grout, every hexagonal cell cut into six kites by six spokes to its side midpoints; an opaque dielectric authored approximation, without real tile dimensions or installation specification certification. (Different from the existing `kisrhombilletiles` 30-60-90 triangles and `trihexagonaltiles` hexagons and triangles.)
- `soroban`: seven-rod soroban — biconical wooden beads on seven bamboo-like rods between frame bars and a reckoning bar showing 4567890 (the last seven digits of the page caption's 1234567890), with a dot on every third rod; an opaque dielectric authored approximation, without real abacus dimensions, materials or teaching correctness certification. (Different from the existing `seedbeads` seed beads.)
- `ean13label`: EAN-13 barcode label — the 30 black bars of the page's example number 4 003994 155486 printed on pale paper, with longer start, centre and end guard bars; an opaque dielectric authored approximation, without GS1 specification, scanner readability or print quality certification. (Different from the existing `papertape` punched paper tape.)
- `embossedbraille`: embossed braille paper — a cream paper face of 7 × 4 braille cells whose smoothly raised round dots spell the letters a to z in order (w skipped, three blank cells at the end); an opaque dielectric authored approximation, without paper material, dot spacing standards or readability certification. (Different from the existing `tactilepaving` regular truncated warning studs.)
- `rule30`: Rule 30 cellular automaton — a 33 × 33 cell tile showing 33 generations of Wolfram's elementary cellular automaton Rule 30 grown from a single black cell in the middle of the top row (time running down), black cells as raised glossy dark squares and white cells as lower, rougher pale squares separated by recessed fine grooves; an opaque dielectric authored approximation, without material, size standards or randomness certification. (Different from the existing `sierpinskicarpet`, `perfectmaze` and similar patterns.)
- `perfectmaze`: seeded perfect maze — an 8 × 8 cell maze whose recessed, dark, smoother corridors run between pale, rougher raised walls, the 64 cells joined by 63 links so every cell is reachable and no loop exists, a different maze for every seed; an opaque dielectric authored approximation, without material, size standards or difficulty guarantees. (Different from `moorecurve`'s single closed groove loop.)
- `sevensegment`: seven-segment display panel — a dark glass face in a 5 × 2 digit grid showing 0123456789, lit amber chamfered bars following the common seven-segment patterns and dim ghosts for the unlit segments; an opaque dielectric authored approximation, without LED/LCD components, emission or standards. (Different from `chipresistors`' white 103 marking.)
- `ulamspiral`: Ulam prime spiral — a 15 × 15 cell tile holding the integers 1-225 in a square spiral (1 at the center), the prime cells marked by glossy dark raised domes on a rougher pale non-metallic slab with recessed cell grooves; an opaque dielectric authored approximation, without material, standards or mathematical certification. (Different from the existing `tactilepaving` regular truncated studs and `dominoes` 0-6 pips.)
- `papertape`: punched paper tape — one inch of eight-level tape (ten 0.1 inch character rows by nine hole tracks) in a pale non-metallic paper that encodes the ASCII text PAPER TAPE in holes (bit 7 as even parity), with a row of smaller sprocket holes nearer the narrow edge and dark backing showing through the rougher holes; an opaque dielectric authored approximation, without material, paper thickness, translucency or standards. (Different from the existing `punchcard` 2 × 4 cards with rectangular punches.)
- `moorecurve`: Moore-curve groove tile — one closed, continuous dark recessed groove looping through every cell of an 8 × 8 cell pale non-metallic tile (a Hamiltonian loop of four rotated Hilbert curves), rougher than the face; an opaque dielectric authored approximation, without material, engraving process or size standards. (Different from the existing groove, maze and tiled presets.)
- `sierpinskicarpet`: Sierpinski carpet stone tile — four recursive levels of recessed squares on an 81 × 81 cell tile (1 of 27 cells, 8 of 9, 64 of 3 and 512 of 1), larger ones deeper and darker, repeating seamlessly; an opaque dielectric authored approximation, a finite four-level carpet rather than the limit set, without stone species, carving process or size standards. (Different from the existing perforated, panelled and pitted presets.)
- `chladnifigure`: Chladni figure — pale sand collected along the nodal lines of a standing-wave pattern on a dark, glossier non-metallic plate, the sand slightly raised and rougher, the line pattern repeating exactly with the UV tile; an opaque dielectric authored approximation that does not model physical plate modes, material or sand-grain physics. (Different from the existing sand, stone and perforated-plate presets.)
- `kisrhombilletiles`: kisrhombille (V4.6.12) glazed tiles — every hexagon cut into twelve congruent 30-60-90 right triangles that alternate deep teal and cream around its center, all straight edges on six line families 30° apart, each face with narrow bevelled edges inside rougher gray grout; an opaque dielectric authored approximation, without tile thickness, glaze chemistry or size standards. (Different from the existing `rhombille`, 3.6.3.6 hexagon–triangle and right-isosceles `righttriangletiles` presets, and the dual of `truncatedtrihexagonaltiles`.)
- `truncatedtrihexagonaltiles`: truncated trihexagonal (4.6.12) glazed tiles — cream dodecagons, small indigo squares turned in three directions 60° apart and lavender hexagons, each with narrow bevelled edges inside rougher gray grout; an opaque dielectric authored approximation, without tile thickness, glaze chemistry or size standards. (A different vertex type from the existing 3.12.12 dodecagon, hexagon–triangle and `rhombitrihexagonaltiles` tile presets.)
- `elongatedtriangulartiles`: elongated triangular (3.3.3.4.4) glazed tiles — upright terracotta square rows alternating with cream rows of up and down triangles, square rows offset by half an edge, each face with narrow bevelled edges inside rougher gray grout; an opaque dielectric authored approximation, without tile thickness, glaze chemistry or size standards. (Same three-triangle, two-square vertex content as `snubsquaretiles` but in a different order, and unlike the existing hexagon–triangle tile presets.)
- `rhombitrihexagonaltiles`: rhombitrihexagonal (3.4.6.4) glazed tiles — cream hexagons, slate-blue squares turned in three directions 60° apart and navy equilateral triangles, each with narrow bevelled edges inside rougher gray grout; an opaque dielectric authored approximation, without tile thickness, glaze chemistry or size standards. (A different vertex type from the existing `snubsquaretiles`, hexagon–triangle and 3.12.12 dodecagon tile presets.)
- `snubsquaretiles`: snub square (3.3.4.3.4) glazed tiles — dark blue squares alternately turned ±15° and cream equilateral triangles, each with narrow bevelled edges inside rougher gray grout; an opaque dielectric authored approximation, without tile thickness, glaze chemistry or size standards. (A different vertex type from the existing hexagon–triangle, Cairo-pentagon and right-triangle tile presets.)
- `starfish`: orange five-armed sea stars on a rougher gray-sand host, a rounded central disc with five arms tapering from base to tip, slightly irregular arm angles and lengths, a raised spine ridge and three rows of small tubercles; an opaque dielectric authored approximation, without species identification, tube feet, spines, a madreporite, real size or marine-biology data.
- `fishscaleshingles`: beige fish-scale metal shingles in eight courses of twelve semicircular lower-edge lobes, two lobes per shingle, half-lobe staggered courses, under-lap shadows and narrow seams; an opaque dielectric authored approximation, without embossing, metallic reflection, real overlap geometry or specification certification. (A variant of the same scalloped-overlap family as the existing `scallopedslate` and `fishscaletiles`.)
- `waffleweave`: cool grey-white rounded float ridges on a fixed 14×16 lattice enclosing recessed square pockets, with higher weft ridges and small knots at crossings; an opaque dielectric authored approximation, without real warp/weft interlacement, cloth physics, absorbency or fabric specification certification. (A finer variant of the same waffle-cloth family as the existing `wafflecloth`.)
- `halite`: milky cube hopper faces on a rough pale grey-pink salt host, each with a randomly rotated square rim and two concentric stepped-down square terraces; an opaque dielectric authored approximation, without measured crystal dimensions, transparency/dissolution physics, full cube meshes or mineral certification. (A dielectric salt variant of the same rotated nested-square hopper family as the existing `bismuthhoppers`.)
- `calcite`: milky oblique rhomb plates on a rough dark mineral host with about 75-degree parallel edges and nested parallel steps; an opaque dielectric authored approximation, without measured cleavage angles, transparency/birefringence, full rhombohedron meshes or mineral certification.
- `flangenuts`: trimmed hexagonal nuts on circular conical flanges with six straight flats and an open bore over rougher dark steel; the whole map is metallic, without underside serrations, full 3D, internal threads or DIN/ISO certification.
- `capnuts`: hexagonal cap nuts on rougher dark steel backing with six straight flats, trimmed corners and a closed circular dome; the whole map is metallic, unlike the through-bored Hex Nuts, without full 3D, internal threads or DIN/ISO certification.
- `bedfordcord`: khaki warp-direction woven cords with continuous fine grooves and half-staggered flat weft dashes; an opaque dielectric surface approximation without real warp/stuffer interlacement, cloth physics or cotton specification/provenance certification.
- `staurolite`: dark-brown 60-degree crossed crystal prisms with parallel sides and blunt ends on light rough host; four arms share an unstacked top. Opaque dielectric approximation, without full meshes, refraction, schist foliation or mineral/chemical certification.
- `dowelpins`: silver solid headless dowel-pin side elevations with rounded/entry-beveled ends and a shallow cylindrical crown; steel stays separate from opaque nonmetal backing, without full meshes, press-fit physics, load or DIN/ISO certification.
- `europeanbeech`: pale pink-tan fine straight-grained European beech with numerous diffuse shallow longitudinal vessels and minute flush ray flecks; no vessel meshes, species identification, waterproofing, structural or provenance certification.
- `habotai`: pearl-white dense fine 1/1 plain silk with same-dye yarns, shallow relief and soft sheen; an opaque surface approximation without transmission, fold/fiber meshes, cloth physics or composition/weight certification.
- `azurite`: deep-blue broad oblique-ended tabular/prismatic faces with shallow striae and local parallel triples above dark rough host; opaque dielectric, not metal, without complete prisms, refraction or mineral/copper-safety certification.
- `whiteoak`: warm olive-brown quarter-sawn White Oak with broad flush transverse ray flecks and shallow longitudinal vessels—not raised burls; no species identification, waterproofing or structural-performance certification.
- `clevispins`: silver clevis-pin side elevations with a short bilateral head, unthreaded shallow-crowned shaft and one enclosed tail bore; steel and opaque nonmetal backing stay separate, without retainer assembly, through-hole meshes, load or DIN/ISO certification.
- `whipcord`: charcoal single steep coarse ribs, same-dye yarns and continuous three-pick floats, distinct from fine Gabardine, paired Cavalry Twill and transverse Grosgrain; no fiber meshes, garment physics or textile-performance certification.
- `vanadinite`: orange-brown raised hexagonal rims with a smaller aligned recessed opaque hexagonal floor on pale rough mineral backing—not solid flat-topped crystals; no complete prisms, transparency or mineral/lead-safety certification.
- `tabwashers`: flat silver annuli with one round bore and one coplanar straight-ended external lug, separating steel from opaque dielectric bore/backing; no split, spherical/cup profile, bending, assembly or locking-performance certification.
- `whiteash`: pale warm-brown straight ash with coplanar growth bands, concentrated coarse earlywood vessels and finer sparse latewood openings; only vessels recess, without endgrain circles, raised grain or species/physics certification.
- `gibheadkeys`: silver solid gib-head taper-key side elevation with a chamfered raised head, one step and a nearly 1:100 straight shank—not double-rounded parallel or half-moon Woodruff keys; no full 3D key, fit, extraction or standards certification.
- `grosgrain`: cool grey-blue grosgrain with fine dense warp crossing single rounded transverse ribs and smooth two-sided crown falloff—not flat Faille or paired Ottoman cords; no ribbon-edge mesh, transmission or cloth physics.
- `pyromorphite`: varied yellow-green raised flat hexagonal termination faces on pale rough mineral backing; end faces only, not whole prismatic/barrel crystals, transparent optics or mineral/lead-safety certification.
- `kingwood`: polished deep red-brown heartwood with unequal flush purple-black fine longitudinal streaks and sparse recessed vessels—not cream-pink Tulipwood, raised grain or species/legal-sourcing certification.
- `faille`: opaque ivory plain weave with fine dense warp, broad flattened single weft ribs, alternating lift and low interstices—not Ottoman paired cords; no transmission or drape simulation.
- `celestine`: pale-blue broad flat tabular crystals in locally parallel groups with staggered ends on neutral rough host; opaque dielectric, without transmission/refraction or mineral certification.
- `braziliantulipwood`: polished warm-cream wood with unequal flush pink-red longitudinal streaks and sparse recessed vessels—not American Tulip Poplar, raised grain ribs or species/legal-sourcing certification.
- `batiste`: ivory fine-cotton one-over-one plain weave with balanced matching yarns, subtle normal/height and recessed interstices; opaque, without transmission, cloth physics or fiber certification.
- `wulfenite`: varied scattered orange near-square flat raised crystal faces on dark rough mineral backing; opaque dielectric, not transparent crystal mesh or mineral/safety certification.
- `lignumvitae`: authored polished dark olive-brown fine coplanar wood grain with sparse short recessed vessels—not broad zebra stripes, species identification, self-lubrication physics or legal-sourcing certification.
- `sateen`: authored ivory five-harness filling-faced cotton sateen with isolated binders and continuous four-end filling floats in normal/height; not printed twill, cloth physics, directional BRDF or fiber certification.
- `parallelkeys`: solid silver-gray double-rounded parallel keys with flat tops, parallel sides and curved ends on dark dielectric backing—not cylinders, hollow rings or mechanically certified part meshes.
- `smithsonite`: authored apple-green botryoidal relief with joined smoother, brighter crowns and dark rough interstices; opaque dielectric, not transparent crystal geometry or mineral certification.
- `macassarebony`: authored polished dark-brown Macassar ebony with coplanar unequal near-black longitudinal streaks and fine sparse recessed vessels—not grooves or joined boards; no species, wood-physics or legal-sourcing certification.
- `serge`: authored matte navy balanced 2/2 Serge with equally dyed yarns and continuous short floats, using normal/height relief rather than printed diagonals; no fuzzy wool mesh, garment physics or fiber/uniform-grade certification.
- `garterstitch`: warm-cream garter ridges with staggered curved purl crowns and lower return arcs, using actual normal/height relief rather than printed stitch symbols; no complete yarn mesh, elasticity/drape or fiber certification.
- `woodruffkeys`: silver-grey solid Woodruff keys with a straight chord, circular arc and nearly flat raised metallic faces on dark dielectric backing—not coins, triangles, bored washers or an assembly mesh; no DIN/material-grade/safety certification.
- `barite`: pale cream flat tabular-crystal faces in locally parallel triples over a matte brown mineral host—not narrow blue ridged Kyanite, round caps or printing; no transmission, full specimen geometry or mineral/safety certification.
- `surah`: authored warm copper-colored balanced 2/2 fine silk twill with continuous short floats on both axes—not crossing-by-crossing beads or copper metal; no cloth drape, transmission, directional silk sheen or fiber certification.
- `bocote`: golden-brown wood with near-black flowing grain folding into local closed eyes; coplanar pigment and sparse recessed longitudinal vessels—not raised rings, endgrain or species/performance certification.
- `hemimorphite`: sky-blue irregular joined rounded mineral caps with pale recesses and bounded single-layer relief—not printed dots, regular beads or metal; no transmission, complete specimen geometry or mineral certification.
- `monkscloth`: natural-cream coarse 4×4 basket weave with four distinct yarns and continuous four-crossing floats—not 2×2 Hopsack, printing or Oxford double-thread guideline cloth; no composition/count/care certification.
- `bronzite`: polished dark-brown stone with localized differently oriented bronze striations on one dielectric plane—a static appearance approximation, not dynamic schiller or mineral/value certification.
- `makore`: reddish-brown veneer with locally fading coplanar transverse curls and sparse recessed longitudinal vessels—not uniform washboard stripes or an endgrain diagram; no optical/species/mechanical certification.
- `redberyl`: raspberry-red raised flat hexagonal crystal faces on pale granular rhyolite, viewed end-on—not pointed quartz, six-lobed flowers or printing; no crystal-column mesh/refraction or mineral/value certification.
- `cottondrill`: warm-khaki matte cotton drill with matching yarn dye and continuous three-pick warp floats—not plain/basket weave, navy stripes or printed lines; no composition/GSM/durability/care certification.
- `bluelaceagate`: polished pale-blue agate with unequal lacy white companion laminae on one plane—not cobalt stone, equal stripes or raised ridges; no mineral/treatment/optics/healing certification.
- `linchpins`: silver closed round-keep Linch Pins with a thicker solid shank and raised head, metallic hardware and dielectric clearances—not R-clips, flat washers or printing; no dimensional/load/safety certification.
- `silknoil`: natural-cream matte Silk Noil with alternating plain yarns and tiny raised neps in both directions—not shiny Shantung, printed flecks or unwashed raw fibers; no fiber/care certification.
- `bloodwood`: vivid-red fine longitudinal Bloodwood grain with sparse tiny recessed vessels, coplanar pigment and dielectric finish—not orange Padauk or red paint; no species/color/aging certification.
- `cavansite`: raised brilliant-blue short-acicular rosettes on a warm pale granular host, smoother but dielectric—not polished blue beads or long-spiked stars; no mineral or crystal-optics certification.
- `matelasse`: warm-ivory woven-quilt cloth with continuous raised diamond binding, softly lofted panels and alternating yarn crowns—not dashed sewing or printed diamonds; no fiber/care certification.
- **Yellowheart**: uniform golden longitudinal wood with subtle fine grain and sparse shallow vessel traces, not neon paint, raised bands or endgrain.
- **R-clips**: single-loop rounded spring wire, a long straight leg and a bowed flared short leg with open dielectric clearances, not closed washers or parallel U-pins.
- **Ticking**: navy broad-center and fine flank stripes woven into dense 2/1 twill with cream weft, not solid printed or embossed bars.
- **Sodalite**: granular blue-violet matrix with filled white mineral veinlets on one polished plane, not gold flecks, ridges or open cracks.
- **Dalmatian stone**: irregular dark green-black inclusions in a creamy polished matrix, flush rather than pits or raised pebbles.
- **Purpleheart**: exposed eggplant-purple long grain with sparse recessed vessels; a static wood appearance, not fluorescence or animated aging.
- **Longitudinal Black Palm**: dense dark fiber bundles in tan ground tissue on one satin face, without endgrain dots, growth rings or knots.
- **Stainless E clips**: three inward contacts and broad wing windows joined to an open mouth, above opaque nonmetal backing.
- `auto` tries WebGPU → WebGL2 → Canvas2D at initialization only. Forced backends do not switch. Runtime GPU/GL recovery uses the same backend; recreate renderer-owned targets and snapshots.
- Canvas2D is 2D-only and rejects native shader effects. Check `game.graphics.capabilities`; see [current contracts](docs/CURRENT.md) for feature-specific limits.
- Unlock audio with `await game.audio.unlock()` inside a user gesture. Game pause does not pause audio. WebGPU/AudioWorklet need HTTPS or localhost; if OPM cannot start, samples and streams still play, so check `game.audio.opmState === 'ready'` before playing OPM assets.
- Deploy the complete `dist/` tree, including vendors, licenses, workers and worklets. Do not mix CommonJS and ESM engine instances. `build:site` produces the complete static site in `.vite/site/`. The opt-in `opmModuleUrl` can load OPM from a commit-pinned CDN instead, without integrity verification (see the technical reference).
- Managed-browser checks are not Safari, physical-device, audible-output or universal performance certification. See [ACCEPTANCE](ACCEPTANCE.md) for evidence and remaining limitations, and the [technical reference](docs/TECHNICAL.md) for API and resource ownership.

## 日本語

XYZ.js はブラウザーのネイティブ API を使う TypeScript ゲームエンジンです。2D／3D 描画、Scene、アニメーション、物理、ナビゲーション、UI、入力、アセット、音声を提供します。公開入口は `xyz.js` で、ECS は内部実装です。ビジュアルエディターや three.js／PixiJS／Excalibur の互換置換ではありません。npm runtime dependencies はなく、同梱 vendor のライセンスを保持します。

**まず動かす：** Node.js ≥22.13.0 で `npx pnpm@12.6.0 install`、続いて `npx pnpm@12.6.0 examples` を実行し、`http://127.0.0.1:5173/examples/` を開きます。npm は未公開のため、自分のプロジェクトには GitHub Release の tarball、またはローカルで build／pack したものをインストールしてください。導入・2D／3D starter・配信手順は [English guide](docs/USAGE.md) を参照してください。

- プロシージャル PBR 材質は自然・建築・工業・生活用品の表面（淡いクリーム色の細かく研磨した長い直木目のバスウッド、凸球面の座面を上に向けた丸穴ステンレス球面座金、緑色の種皮に細い深緑の輪と長い白いへそを持つ丸ごとの乾燥緑豆、平らな研磨面のピンクと乳白色の同心眼紋を持つThomsonite、暖かい銀白色で不規則な細いヘアラインを持つ洋白合金板、均一なベージュに染めた連続浮き糸のLyocell 3/1綾織、暖かい赤褐色の細い木目に不規則な暗色長縞を持つTigerwood研磨面、微細なピンク色と淡色の鉱物斑が混ざるThulite研磨面、短い淡色のへそを黒い楕円の目が囲む乳白色の乾燥ササゲ、半径方向に一貫した三つの波と中央開口を持つ銀色の閉環ウェーブワッシャー、細い縦の色模様と疎な導管を持つ黄金褐色のイロコ、オフホワイトのラミー平織、深い赤紫の結晶片と黒い石基のEudialyte、緑とサーモンピンクが細粒状に混ざる研磨ユナカイト、淡い黄の縦稜と側面の小さな傷跡を持つ白いベニバナの痩果、赤褐色の細い直線木目に不規則な暗い平面斑紋を持つスネークウッド、円形の外周と回転した四角い開口を持つ平らな銀色ワッシャー、暗い地に静的な青灰色の長石光斑が散らばる研磨ラルビカイト、中央の開口・円すい内壁・高い外周リムを持つ金属製カップワッシャー、横方向の縮み杢と細長い導管を持つ淡い金褐色のアニグレ、長い淡色のへそを中央に持つ凸面の小豆、淡い途切れた交差模様を持つ青緑色の研磨アマゾナイト、2つの丸い止まり穴と中央の鋼製ブリッジを持つねじ頭、淡い金色の地に幅の異なる暗い縦縞と疎らな開いた導管を持つゼブラウッド、淡い種枕を一つ持つ光沢のある斑入りヒマ種子、青灰色と金褐色の繊維質角礫を持つ研磨ピーターサイト、4辺が平らな四角い止まり穴を持つ鋼ねじ頭、淡色の方解石で満たされた亀裂と褐色の縁を持つ研磨Septarian断面、中央で3本の溝が交わるY字形の止まり穴を持つねじ頭、暗色地に淡い尖ったマグネサイト結晶が入る研磨Pinolith、短い起毛で平織り地を覆う綿フランネル、粗い開放導管を持つ赤橙色のパドゥーク木目、平らな鋼製底を持つ5つの丸いペンタローブ穴のねじ頭、不規則な縦しわを持つ黒褐色のトンカ豆、細かな流理と淡い斑晶を持つ流紋岩、スリット付き中空スプリングピンの端面、5本の淡い縦稜を持つ三日月形キャラウェイ果実、白黒の粗い結晶を持つ閃緑岩、濃淡の緑と微細な交錯鉱物模様を持つ不透明ネフライト、青い経糸と白い緯糸のシャンブレー平織り、赤褐色のサペリのリボン杢、閉じた両端と縦溝を持つ細長いデーツの種、六角の止まり穴を持つ丸いボルト頭、深緑に銀白の羽状鉱物模様、細い果柄を持つCubebの乾燥果、淡色の双晶斜長岩、5ピックの浮き経糸を持つHuckabackタオル地、空洞と裂け目のある赤い花椒の果皮、青緑色の結節群を持つクリソコラ、二つの背溝と片側研磨刃を持つ銀色台形カッター替刃、多孔質ミネラル結合材と麻芯片のヘンプクリート、不規則な太節緯糸を持つ暖色アイボリーのシャンタン、細かな波状片理と雲母斑を持つ灰緑色の千枚岩、角張った細凹表皮の炭黒色ニゲラ種子、開口リングと先細りねじ軸を備えた銀色スクリューフック、二本の急斜畝を持つオリーブ色キャバリーツイル、乳白色の雲状下地と浅い毛孔を持つ羊皮紙、細長い開放導管孔を持つ濃褐色ウェンジ材、紫色の角形成長色帯を持つ研磨蛍石、二本の湾曲した先細り角を持つ黒褐色の菱の実殻、U字弓・横ピン・穴付きピンタブを備えた銀色シャックル、太い緯糸を対に束ねた横畝オットマン織物、白い鉱物塊と細い灰色網脈のハウライト、長い曲線状の靭皮繊維を持つ手漉き桑紙、五つの取付穴と二重通風穴を持つブレーキディスク、縦積みの細長い凹面翡翠色タイル、八方向の暗い種を持つ平らな柿の切断面、褐色の鉄結晶斑と明るい縁を持つ油滴黒釉、高密度の経二上一下急斜文ギャバジン、尖った葉身と湾曲した側脈を持つ月桂樹の葉、ピンクと白の波状層帯を持つ菱マンガン鉱、両眼ねじ付きターンバックル、暗い粗粒と淡い双晶斜長石を持つ研磨された斑れい岩、青灰色のブルームと三本の低い放射状の継ぎ目を持つジュニパーベリー、対になった高い経畝と細かい平織を持つ象牙色のディミティ綿布、暖かな四象限と内部の深色十字を持つキアストライト研磨面、二つの研磨長面と三つの丸い部分がつながる中央溝を持つ銀色両刃カミソリ、幅広い深色の流紋と細い木目を持つ橙赤色のココボロ心材、連続した縦の隆起と丸い疣を持つゴーヤーの皮、対称S曲線で閉じる青緑色のオジーランタンタイル、同心皮殻と暗い核を持つ研磨された魚卵状石灰岩、細い経糸と太い緯糸が一対一で交差する綿ポプリン、七つの台形歯と二つの低い取付孔を持つ銀色ラック、連続した赤い皮と先細りの曲がった果毛を持つランブータン、閉じた真鍮の六弁枠と青緑エナメルの有線七宝、連続した丸い帯のgyroid断面インフィル、片側の五つの孔と高い縁を持つ凹型のアワビ殻内面、濃色の布に淡いY字の糸を配したフライステッチ、六面の棘と連続した下地を持つドリアンの皮、六つの釘穴と連続した低い溝を持つ金属の蹄鉄、下縁が巻き上がる薄片と繊維状の下地を持つペーパーバーク、青い鉱物の雲と淡い曲線状の繊維脈が同じ研磨面に広がるラリマー、縦の幅広い植物片を横の下層に圧着した細長い繊維のパピルス紙、暖かな木目に不規則な淡い放射組織斑を持つレースウッド、二本ずつの青い経糸と淡い緯糸が交互に上下するオックスフォード織、実際に上下が交互になる二本の丸い銀線・高い巻き輪・各結び目からつながる四本の尖った返しを持つ継ぎ目のない有刺鉄線、二つの本当に渦巻く低いカラメル層・中央の折り目・切れ込んだ二つの山・先細りの下端を持つ黄金色のハート形パルミエ、暖かな連続木目に彫り込んだ鏡像一対の三次曲線f字孔・丸い両端・左右の切欠き、本当に開いた閉環アイ・連続した軸・下半円の曲がり・先細りの針先とかえしを持つ銀色J字釣り針、同じ根元につながる三本の曲がった赤い糸・幅広の丸い先端・二つの開いた分岐隙間を持つサフラン、閉じた銀色の縁・本当に低く開いた空洞・高い不透明な黒い舌片・さらに高い四つの銀色接点を持つ矩形ソケットなど）を扱います。色調整 `tint`、`workerPool` での Worker 生成、`cache` による再利用、parallax 用の任意の `heightMap` に対応します。
- **Hopsack**：濃紺の2×2バスケット織材質。経緯とも二本組で、連続した浮き糸と各糸の間の溝を表現します。
- **スノーフレーク・オブシディアン**：黒い研磨面に、大小の灰白色の放射状結晶模様を平らに表現します。
- `f05opticalfaces`：キャップなしJIS-F05光受信器の黒い樹脂製矩形正面４個。長い側溝、片端２角を面取りした六辺の凸ガイドと丸い側突起２個、暗いキー形開口と低い丸穴を表します。全て非金属で、シャッター・LED・透明レンズはありません。NRNDのTORX1350A(F)水平型図面の作者近似で、光学／データ／音声／電気／嵌合／EMC／安全のシミュレーションや認証ではありません。当時880種類。
- `minidin4femalefaces`：mini-DIN４接点メス正面を2×2配置。黒PBTの台座／円形内芯、連続した薄い錫めっき真鍮の接地環、上の開放切欠き、下側面２段差、上縦／下横の４スロットと独立した下の閉じた矩形キー穴。[Same Sky MD-Sの２ページ](https://www.sameskydevices.com/product/resource/md-s.pdf)の独立MD-40S正面図と[メーカー写真](https://cdn.sameskydevices.com/products/image/getproductimage/976?typecode=m&width=300)に基づき、汎用９接点図やPCB穴配置とは区別します。金属は接地環のみ。既定metallic1／明示0は保存maskを変更しません。外部シールド缶・可視の銀色内部接点環・映像／電気模擬はありません。形状／PBRは作者近似でCAD／嵌合／認証ではありません。512推奨、32は粗いプレビュー。当時881種類。
- `dcbarreljackfaces`：DCバレルジャック正面４個を2×2配置。縦長の黒PBT矩形、上寄りの暗い丸口、奥まった中実ニッケル中心ピンと独立した下側の銀色横帯（上下は平ら、両端のみ丸口で切り取り）。金属M1はピン／横帯のみ、他のPBT／穴底はM0。奥行きは背面 < 穴底 < 横帯 < ピン < 台座面で、外側金属環や追加の凸円枠はありません。[Same Sky PJ-102A](https://www.sameskydevices.com/product/interconnect/connectors/dc-power-connectors/jacks/pj-102a)・[メーカー図２ページ](https://www.sameskydevices.com/product/resource/pj-102a.pdf)・[正面写真](https://cdn.sameskydevices.com/products/image/getproductimage/690?typecode=m&width=300)に基づく作者近似。既定metallic1／明示0はfactorのみ変更し保存maskを変えません。背面はんだ脚・PCB・実体貫通穴を省略し、元資料は同梱しません。CAD／電気／極性／スイッチ／安全／嵌合の模擬・認証ではありません。512推奨、32は粗いプレビュー。当時882種類。
- `stfiberadapterfaces`：ST simplex正面４個を2×2配置した簡略図。六平面のめっき金属六角台座、同心の丸い肩、上に１個だけ開いた軸方向キー溝を持つ凸円筒リップ、左右対向２個のバヨネット突起、深く暗い不透明な円形空洞。キー溝はリップだけを連続した金属肩まで下げ、背景へ貫通しません。金具／溝底M1、空洞／背面M0。深さは背面 < 穴底 < 六角台座 < 肩／キー底 < 突起 < リップ。[SENKO ST](https://www.senko.com/product/st-adapter-2/)・５ページの[DS-ST-000006-RevA-ENの１／３ページ](https://www.senko.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/01/DS-ST-000006-RevA-EN.pdf)・[メーカー写真](https://www.senko.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/09/ST-Adapter.png)に基づく216-101の作者近似。めっき金属のみ既知でニッケルとは断定しません。内部zirconiaは仕様にありますが白環は明瞭でなく、陶磁環／中心接点／ピンは描きません。29.3 mmは軸方向長さ、double-D 9.6 × 7.8 mmはパネル穴参考で正面形状ではありません。metadata作成日2024-11-15は印刷日ではありません。CADではなく、ねじ／ローレット歯／座金／キャップ／背部／ケーブル／ferrule／PCBを省略。光学／電気／嵌合／安全模擬やbrowser／FPS認証ではありません。0.04 m／0.0042 UV relief、既定metallic1、明示0はfactorのみ。constructorでキャッシュし、queryに配置／ループ／hash／trig／sqrtなし。当時883種類。
- `ftypefemalefaces`：F型メスのバルクヘッド正面４個を2×2配置。六平面のニッケルめっき金属六角台座、同心の凸円肩、閉じたキー溝なしの凸円形リップ、淡色P.P.誘電体円盤とさらに奥の小さく暗い不透明な中央入口。外部金具M1、背面／P.P.／入口M0。深さは背面 < 入口 < 六角台座 < 肩 < P.P. < リップ。STのキー溝／突起、オスピン、架空の可視中央金属接点環はありません。歴史資料[Amphenol Connex CAT-103 ©2001、PDF159／印刷158ページ](https://docs.rs-online.com/1ab7/A700000006717073.pdf#page=159)の222121写真／図面とNickel／P.P.／75Ω表に基づき、汎用Delrin／Teflonではありません。11 HEX・軸方向全長21.3 mm・座金／ナット側面寸法は参考で、正面高さの実測値ではありません。微小入口の実際の断面は判別できず、円形は作者の簡略化。既定0.04 m tile／0.0042 UV relief（メートル深さではない）、metallic1／明示0はfactorのみで保存mask不変。ねじ螺旋／装飾ねじ環、座金歯、背部／ケーブル、実体穴／透明／新meshを省略。材質・光学は作者近似でCAD／RF／75Ω／電気／嵌合／安全模擬ではなく、75Ωは歴史的な部品識別のみ。constructorでキャッシュしqueryに割り当て／ループ／hash／trig／sqrtなし。既存pbr3dとtexture所有権を維持。512推奨、32は粗いプレビュー。当時884種類。
- `to220packagefaces`：外露金属タブのTO-220正面４個を2×2配置。タブ上部の両２角を面取りし、暗い不透明な凹取付穴１個、艶消し黒の矩形本体、等間隔で平行な直線リード３本（広い根元肩／細い尾）を描きます。タブ／脚M1、他M0、C1境界混合で電気的絶縁を意味しません。作者高さは背面.02 < 穴.06 < タブ.30 < 脚.44 < 本体.68。[STP55NF06 May 2012 Doc ID7544 Rev11](https://www.st.com/resource/en/datasheet/stb55nf06.pdf#page=13)の１ページ表紙と13ページFigure23を実見、12ページ寸法表を実読。TO-220FPではありません。寸法は参考のみでCAD／熱／電気／RF／安全／FPS模擬ではなく、化学成分やめっきを推定しません。既定0.08 m tile／0.0042 UV relief（メートル深さではない）／metallic1、明示0はfactorだけを変更しmask不変。typed cache73,856 BはJS開銷を除外、queryに割り当て／ループ／hash／trig／sqrt／近傍探索なし。512推奨、32は細い脚の解像を保証しません。目録885種類、plain込みgallery886項。
- `robinsonschensted`：ロビンソン・シェンステッド対応。3つの順列（恒等・反転・ランダム・ランダムな対合・シフト・2倍 mod 11）それぞれの順列行列、挿入盤 P、記録盤 Q（同じ形の標準ヤング盤、数字は 1〜10）を、順列ごとに1行3枚のパネルで描きます。濃い梅色の地にマゼンタ色で、seed % 6 が1行目の種類を、⌊seed / 6⌋ が種類の間隔とランダムなキーを選びます。完全な周期模様で、模式図であり、`permutations`・`standardtableaux`・`youngdiagrams`の概念を結びつけます（概念上は重なります）。テストは画面から読み戻し、逆バンピングで順列を復元し、シェンステッドの定理（1行目＝最長増加部分列、行数＝最長減少部分列）とシュッツェンベルジェの対称性（P＝Q は対合のときに限る）を確認し、インク・height・色調・occlusion を点ごとに比較します。現在のカタログは1182種で、plain を含む gallery は1183項目です。

- `friezegroups`：フリーズ群。8本の横方向の帯（1本8行、64×64マス）で、各帯は対称性を全く持たない3×3の図形を7つのフリーズ群（p1・p11g・p1m1・p11m・p2・p2mg・p2mm）のいずれかに従って繰り返したものです。7つの群が1本ずつと、追加の群が1本あり、順序は混ぜられます。濃い緑の地にミント色で、seed % 6 が図形を、⌊seed / 6⌋ が順序と追加の群を選びます。完全な周期模様で、模式図であり、`wallpapergroups`の1次元版（同じ対称群の仲間）です。テストは画面から、すべての平行移動・鏡映・半回転・滑り鏡映を試して各帯の対称群を求め、7つの群が1本ずつと追加の1本、およびその周期を確認し、インク・height・色調・occlusion を点ごとに比較します。現在のカタログは1181種で、plain を含む gallery は1182項目です。

- `huffmancodes`：ハフマン符号。同じ16個の重み（英字のおよその頻度・Zipf・フィボナッチ数・2のべき・すべて等しい・平方数）に対するハフマン符号とシャノン・ファノ符号を、2行×2枚のパネルに並べます。1行が1つの記号で、ビット0は暗く1は明るく表します。深い茶色の地に青銅色で、seed % 6 が上の行の重みを、⌊seed / 6⌋ が下の行の重みを選びます。完全な周期模様で、模式図です。テストは画面から読み戻し、語頭条件（プレフィックス符号）・クラフト和が1であること・ハフマン符号の総長が最小であること（別に書いた併合法）・平均長がエントロピーとエントロピー＋1の間にあること・シャノン・ファノがハフマンより良くないこと・フィボナッチ数と2のべきの階段の長さを確認し、インク・height・色調・occlusion を点ごとに比較します。現在のカタログは1180種で、plain を含む gallery は1181項目です。

- `burrowswheeler`：バロウズ・ホイーラー変換。24文字の2つのテキスト（フィボナッチ語、トゥエ・モース語、mississippi・banana・abracadabra の繰り返し、ランダムな4文字）の24個の回転とソートした回転を、テキストごとに1行2枚のパネルで描き、最後の列が変換結果です。濃い藍色の地にラベンダー色で、seed % 6 が上のテキストを、⌊seed / 6⌋ が下のテキストとランダムなテキストのキーを選びます。完全な周期模様で、模式図であり、変種0と1のテキストは`fibonacciword`・`thuemorse`と同じ語ですが、描くのは回転行列です。テストは画面から読み戻し、回転、接尾辞順に並べて別に書いた結果との一致、最後の列から LF 写像で元のテキストが復元されることを確認し、インク・height・色調・occlusion を点ごとに比較します。現在のカタログは1179種で、plain を含む gallery は1180項目です。

- `lightsoutpuzzles`：ライツアウトのパズル。5・6・7・9 の盤面で、各行2枚のパネルに点灯した盤面と最少手数の解を並べた2つのパズルを描きます（GF(2) 上のガウスの消去法で、静かなパターンがあるときはすべての解のうち最も軽いものを採用）。パズルは消灯した盤面でパターン（中心・十字・全部・市松・外周・対角線）を押して作ります。濃い茶色の地にクリーム色で、seed % 6 が上のパズルのパターンを、⌊seed / 6⌋ が大きさと下のパズルのパターンを選びます。完全な周期模様で、模式図です。テストは画面から読み戻し、押下を直接シミュレートしてすべての灯りが消えることを確認し、ライトチェイシングですべての解を列挙して画面の解の手数が最少であることを確認し（5 では4個、9 では256個）、インク・height・色調・occlusion を点ごとに比較します。現在のカタログは1178種で、plain を含む gallery は1179項目です。

- `pseudoprimeliars`：擬素数の嘘つき表。64×64 個の角丸タイルで、行は奇数 n（521〜8,997 の8つの窓で、前の7つにはそれぞれカーマイケル数が1つ含まれる）、列は底 a で、各マスは a が n の Fermat・Euler・強（Miller–Rabin）・Euler–Jacobi の各テストを通るかどうかを表します。n と公約数を持つ、証人、n が素数のときの嘘つき、n が合成数のときの嘘つき（擬素数）の4種類です。濃い紺の地に氷青色で、seed % 4 がテストを、⌊seed / 4⌋ が窓と底の範囲を選びます。完全な周期模様で、模式図であり、`multiplicativeorder`と同じ (n, a) 表の仲間（Fermat の嘘つきは乗法的位数と関係する）ですが、描くものは異なります。テストは画面から読み戻し、BigInt の冪剰余・試し割りによる素因数分解・ルジャンドル記号の積で別に書いた判定とマス単位で比較し、素数に証人がないこと、強 ⊆ Euler–Jacobi ⊆ Euler ⊆ Fermat の嘘つき、カーマイケル数（Korselt）の行に Fermat の証人がないことを確認し、インク・height・色調・occlusion を点ごとに比較します。現在のカタログは1177種で、plain を含む gallery は1178項目です。

- `finitefieldtables`：有限体の演算表。GF(4)・GF(8)・GF(9)・GF(16)・GF(5)・GF(7) の加法表と乗法表を、2行×2枚のパネルに並べます（上下の行は同じ体の2通りの要素の名付け）。要素は多項式の係数で名付け、名前0と1が零元と単位元です。濃い紺の地に金色で、seed % 6 が体を、⌊seed / 6⌋ が2回のシャッフルを選びます。完全な周期模様で、模式図であり、演算が1つの群の乗積表`cayleytable`とは異なります。テストは画面から4つの表を読み戻して、体の公理すべて（単位元・可換・結合・分配・ラテン方陣性・逆元・零因子なし）、標数、乗法群が巡回群であること、2通りの名付けで位数の分布が同じことを確認し、インク・height・色調・occlusion を点ごとに比較します。現在のカタログは1176種で、plain を含む gallery は1177項目です。

- `minesweeperboards`：マインスイーパーの盤面。解き終えた盤面（9×9・12×12・16×16・8×8）で、地雷でないマスは周囲の地雷の数を 3×3 のサイコロの目として外周に沿って時計回りに点灯し（0〜8個）、地雷は9個すべてが点いた明るく高い台座です。地雷はハッシュキーが最も小さいマスで、濃い栗色の地にコーラル色です。seed % 6 が盤を、⌊seed / 6⌋ が地雷の位置を選びます。完全な周期模様で、模式図であり、振られたサイコロの`gamedice`とは異なります。テストは画面から地雷の一覧を読み戻し、別の方法（各地雷の隣接マスに1を加える）で数を計算して目と比べ、地雷の数とグループごとの違いを確認し、台座と目の色調・height・occlusion を点ごとに比較します。現在のカタログは1175種で、plain を含む gallery は1176項目です。

- `beattysequences`：ビーティ数列の補対。連続する整数を行ごとに w×w（24・32・34・40）の角丸タイルに並べ、⌊kα⌋ の項を明るく、⌊kβ⌋（1/α + 1/β = 1）の項を暗くします。レイリーの定理によりどのマスもちょうど一方に属します。α は φ・√2・1 + √2・√3・√5・2 + √3 で、深い青緑の地に水色です。seed % 6 が α を、⌊seed / 6⌋ が幅と開始位置を選びます。完全な周期模様で、模式図であり、φ の場合は`fibonacciword`と同じ種類の図です（全く新しい概念ではありません）。テストは画面から読み戻し、整数演算と整数平方根だけで別に書いた判定とマス単位で比較し、どのマスもちょうど1つの数列に属することと第1の数列の密度が約 1/α であることを確認し、インク・height・色調・occlusion を点ごとに比較します。現在のカタログは1174種で、plain を含む gallery は1175項目です。

- `diophantinegrid`：ディオファントス方程式の格子。64×64 個の角丸タイルが整数の組 (a, b) を表し、a² + b²、a² + ab + b²、a² − ab + b²、(a² + b²)/2、a² + 3b²、a² − b²（a > b）のいずれかがちょうど平方数のとき光ります。原始解（a と b が互いに素）が最も明るく、炭色の地に銀白色で、seed % 6 が式を選び、⌊seed / 6⌋ が窓を動かします。完全な周期模様で、模式図であり、`gcdtable`（同じ (a, b) の方格）と重なりますが、平方数の部分集合だけを描き原始解を区別します。テストは画面から読み戻して別に書いた判定とマス単位で比較し、ユークリッドの公式で原始ピタゴラス解、倍にしても閉じていること、対称性を確認し、インク・height・色調・occlusion を点ごとに比較します。現在のカタログは1173種で、plain を含む gallery は1174項目です。

- `transformbasis`：変換の基底画像。2次元の余弦変換（DCT-II、N = 4・5・6・8）とハールウェーブレット変換（N = 4・8）の N×N 枚の基底画像を、N×N のパネル（各パネルは N×N の角丸ピクセル）に並べます。色調と height は (値 + 1)/2 で決まり、パネルの間は隙間を広くします。濃い紫の地にピンク色で、seed % 6 が種類を選び、⌊seed / 6⌋ のビットがパネルの行と列の入れ替えと符号の反転を選びます。完全な周期模様で、模式図であり、`walshmatrix`、`zernikemodes` とは異なります。テストは画面から各ピクセルの値を読み戻して定義と比べ（誤差 1e-9 未満）、すべての画像が互いに直交すること、二乗ノルム、ハール画像の値が −1・0・1 だけであること、パーセバルの等式を確認し、インク・height・色調・occlusion を点ごとに比較します。現在のカタログは1172種で、plain を含む gallery は1173項目です。

- `hanoistates`：ハノイの塔の状態。最短解のすべての状態を1枚のパネルにし（2・4・6枚の円盤を3本目または2本目の杭へ、状態は4・16・64個）、3本の杭の上の円盤を、中央に置いた棒として描きます。幅・色調・height は円盤の大きさで決まります。濃い緑の地にライム色で、seed % 6 が種類を選び、⌊seed / 6⌋ のビットが杭の左右反転とパネルの順序の反転を選びます。完全な周期模様で、模式図であり、`graycode` とは異なります。テストは画面から各パネルの積み重ねを読み戻して別に書いた再帰と比べ、各状態が正当であること、2^枚数 個の状態がすべて異なること、最初と最後の状態が正しい杭にあること、各ステップで一番上の円盤を動かすこと、第 t ステップで動く円盤が 1 + t の末尾の0の数であることを確認し、インク・height・色調・occlusion を点ごとに比較します。現在のカタログは1171種で、plain を含む gallery は1172項目です。

- `euclideanrhythms`：ユークリッドのリズム。k 個の拍を n ステップの円周上にできるだけ均等に配置したリズム E(k, n) を、k = 1 から n まで1行ずつ並べます。拍のあるステップは次の拍までの間隔（短いほうか長いほう）で色と高さが決まり、拍のないステップは淡く低いタイルです。n = 8・12・16・20・24・32 で、濃い茶色の地に琥珀色です。seed % 6 が種類を、⌊seed / 6⌋ が k 行目を グループ·k ステップ左へ回す量を選びます。完全な周期模様で、模式図であり、`necklaces` とは異なります。テストは画面から各行を読み戻して、別に書いたビョルクルンドのアルゴリズムの結果の回転であること、グループ0が「⌊(i + 1)k/n⌋ が ⌊ik/n⌋ より大きい」という式に等しいこと、各行に k 個の拍があり間隔が2種類だけで、長い間隔が n mod k 個、円周上のどの窓でも拍の数が高々1しか違わないことを確認し、インク・height・色調・occlusion を点ごとに比較します。現在のカタログは1170種で、plain を含む gallery は1171項目です。

- `editdistance`：編集距離の表。同じ長さの2つの単語の動的計画法の表（編集距離または最長共通部分列の長さ。intention／execution、recurrence／occurrence、16文字のDNA 2本）で、セルの色調と height は値で決まり、最後のセルから戻る最適経路の1本上のセルは大きく高くなります。濃い緑の地に青緑色で、seed % 6 が種類を、⌊seed / 6⌋ のビットが単語の反転と入れ替えを選びます。完全な周期模様で、模式図であり、`euclidsteps` とは異なります。テストは画面から表全体を読み戻して別に書いた再帰と比べ、各セルが漸化式を満たすこと、入れ替えると転置になること、画面上の経路が別に書いた逆戻りと一致しコストが最後のセルの値に等しいことを確認し、インク・height・色調・occlusion を点ごとに比較します。現在のカタログは1169種で、plain を含む gallery は1170項目です。

- `sortingtraces`：ソートの軌跡。64個の値の配列を、ソートアルゴリズムの各ステップ後の姿で1行ずつ描きます。セルの色調と height は値で決まります。バブル、挿入、選択、ボトムアップのマージ、クイック（ロムート）、ヒープの各ソートです。濃い梅色の地にローズ色で、seed % 6 がアルゴリズムを、⌊seed / 6⌋ が入力（64で割った余りの等差数列）を選びます。完全な周期模様で、模式図であり、データに依存しない `sortingnetwork` とは異なります。テストは画面から64×64全体を読み戻して別に書いた軌跡とセルごとに比べ、各アルゴリズムが各ステップで保証すること（バブルの整列済みの末尾、挿入の整列済みの先頭、選択の最小値、マージの整列済みの区間、クイックの確定した軸の数が減らないこと、ヒープの最大ヒープと整列済みの末尾）を確認し、インク・height・色調・occlusion を点ごとに比較します。現在のカタログは1168種で、plain を含む gallery は1169項目です。

- `golombrulers`：ゴロムルーラー。最適なゴロムルーラー（2つの目盛の距離がすべて異なり、長さが最短。目盛は5〜10個、長さは11・17・25・34・44・55で、自作の探索で見つけたもの）です。セル (i, j) は i と j がどちらも目盛のときに点を持つので、主対角線以外の各対角線には点が高々1つしかありません。色調と height は差 |i − j| で決まります。濃い茶色の地に金色で、seed % 6 が種類を、⌊seed / 6⌋ が同じ目盛数のすべての最適ルーラー（鏡像を含む）から1本を選びます。完全な周期模様で、模式図であり、`costasambiguity`、`projectiveplane`、`nimproduct` とは異なります。テストは画面から目盛を読み戻して、別に書いた探索（9個の目盛まで）と比べ、差がすべて異なること、各対角線の点の数を確認し、インク・height・色調・occlusion を点ごとに比較します。現在のカタログは1167種で、plain を含む gallery は1168項目です。

- `langfordpairings`：ラングフォード配列。1 から n の各数字が2回ずつ現れ、k の2つの端の間に k 個の数字がある配列（ラングフォード配列。n = 3 と n = 4 は各2個、n = 7 は52個）と、2つの端が k 個離れたスコーレム列（n = 4 の6個、n = 5 の10個、n = 8 の504個のうち辞書順で先頭の64個）です。配列ごとに1つのパネル、数字ごとに1本の棒で、色調と height は数字で決まります。濃い緑の地にミント色で、seed % 6 が種類を選び、⌊seed / 6⌋ のビットがパネルの順序と配列の読み方を反転します。完全な周期模様で、模式図であり、`permutations`、`golombruns`、`setpartitions` とは異なります。テストは画面から数字を読み戻して別の方法の列挙と比べ、各数字の2つの端の距離、パネルがすべて異なること、完全な種類の個数を確認し、インク・height・色調・occlusion を点ごとに比較します。現在のカタログは1166種で、plain を含む gallery は1167項目です。

- `mondrianpartitions`：モンドリアン分割。正方形を、どの2つも合同でない整数の長方形に切り分け、色調と height は面積で決まります。5×5・6×6・7×7・8×8・10×10 の面積差が最小の分割（差は 4・5・5・6・8、自作の探索で見つけたもので、n = 10 は最小の保証なし）と、1辺 112 の完全正方形分割（辺の長さがすべて異なる21個の正方形、デュイヴェスティンのもの）です。濃い紺の地に青で、seed % 6 が種類を、⌊seed / 6⌋ mod 8 が回転と反転を選びます。完全な周期模様で、模式図であり、`euclidsteps`、`pentominotiles`、`trominotiling` とは異なります。テストは画面から各ブロック（隙間の線で区切られたもの）を読み戻して、長方形であること、面積の合計、形がすべて異なること、面積差、面積に応じた色調、21個の辺が一覧と一致すること、グループによる回転と反転、縁の滑らかさを確認し、インク・height・色調・occlusion を点ごとに比較します。現在のカタログは1165種で、plain を含む gallery は1166項目です。

- `debruijntorus`：ド・ブラウン・トーラス。端で回り込む記号の格子で、起こりうるすべての窓パターンがちょうど1回ずつ現れます（完全写像）。4×4 の 2 記号 2×2 窓、8×8 と 4×16 の 2 記号 2×3 窓、8×8 の 2 記号 3×2 窓、2つの 9×9 の 3 記号 2×2 窓です。濃い紫の地に薄紫の角丸正方形で、色調と height は記号で決まります。seed % 6 が種類を選び、⌊seed / 6⌋ が格子の平行移動と記号の入れ替えを決めます。完全な周期模様で、模式図であり、格子は作者自身が書いたバックトラック探索で見つけたものです。テストは画面から記号を読み戻して、窓がすべて異なること、小さい窓の出現回数が均等であること、窓を90度回すと一意でなくなること、グループによる移動と記号の入れ替えを確認し、インク・height・色調・occlusion を点ごとに比較します。現在のカタログは1164種で、plain を含む gallery は1165項目です。

- `kneser`：クネーザー行列。n 個の要素のすべての k 部分集合に1行1列を割り当て、セルは角丸の正方形で、色調と height は2つの部分集合が共有する要素の数で決まります（ジョンソン・スキーム）。互いに素なもの（クネーザーグラフの辺）が最も明るく、対角線が最も暗くなります。(n, k) は (5,2)、(6,2)、(7,2)、(6,3)、(7,3)、(8,3) で、濃い茶色の地に琥珀色です。seed % 6 が種類を、⌊seed / 6⌋ mod 3 が並び順（辞書式、要素の和、回転ドア順）を選びます。完全な周期模様で、模式図であり、`projectiveplane` の 0／1 の接続行列とは異なります。テストは各セルの共有数を別の方法で計算し直し、行列が対称であること、各行の共有数ごとの個数が二項係数であること、アソシエーション・スキームの交点数が共有数だけで決まること、seed 0 がペテルセングラフ（強正則 (10,3,0,1)）であること、6 個から 3 個の各部分集合に互いに素なものがその補集合だけであること、回転ドア順で隣り合う行が k−1 個を共有することを確認し、インク・height・色調・occlusion を点ごとに比較します。現在のカタログは1163種で、plain を含む gallery は1164項目です。

- `lowdiscrepancy`：低食い違い点集合。トーラス上の低食い違い数列の最初の数点を、1点ずつ円で描きます。ハルトン数列（底 2・3）144 点、ハマースリー点集合 64 点、フィボナッチ格子 144 点、プラスチック数のクロネッカー数列 144 点、ハルトン数列（底 2・5）80 点と（底 3・5）75 点です。円の色調と height は数列での番号で決まり、数列が正方形をどう埋めていくかが見えます。濃い紺の地にクリーム色です。seed % 6 が種類を、⌊seed / 6⌋ がグループを選び、グループは点全体をトーラス上で平行移動します。完全な周期模様で、模式図であり、ランダムな `poissondisk` や黄金角の `vogelspiral` とは異なります。テストは各点を別の式で計算し直して、各円の中心の height が番号に対応し、格子点上に余分な点も欠けた点もないこと、ハルトンとハマースリーの箱にちょうど1点ずつ入ること、フィボナッチ格子が加法で閉じていることを確認し、インク・height・色調・occlusion を点ごとに比較します。現在のカタログは1162種で、plain を含む gallery は1163項目です。

- `hamiltonpaths`：ハミルトン路。小さな格子点の辺に沿って、すべての点をちょうど1回通る路（逆向きに歩いたものは同じとみなす）を1つずつ描きます。3×2・3×3・4×3・5×3・4×4・6×3 点の 8・20・62・132・276・336 本です。両端に大きな点があり、路の色調と height は曲がる回数で決まります。濃い茶黒の地に珊瑚色です。seed % 6 が種類を、⌊seed / 6⌋ がパネルの並び順を選びます。完全な周期模様で、模式図であり、ランダムな大きな1つの輪を描く `hamiltoncycle` や、決まった歩数の自己回避歩行の `selfavoidingwalks` とは異なります。テストは画面から各パネルの路を読み戻し、各路がすべての点を通る1本の路で重複がないことを確認し、描かれた本数が動的計画法で数えたハミルトン路の総数に等しいことを確認し、インク・height・色調・occlusion を点ごとに比較します。現在のカタログは1161種で、plain を含む gallery は1162項目です。

- `gridsplits`：方眼の2分割。方眼の盤を、それぞれ1つにつながった2つの領域に分ける分け方をすべて1つずつ描きます。4×3・4×4・6×3・5×4 の盤を等しい2つに分ける 19・70・85・245 通り、4×4 を 6 と 10 に分ける 42 通り、5×3 を 7 と 8 に分ける 36 通りです。同じ領域のマスは継ぎ目なくつながり、左上のマスを含む領域は明るく高くなります。濃い緑黒の地に薄緑です。seed % 6 が種類を、⌊seed / 6⌋ がパネルの並び順を選びます。完全な周期模様で、模式図であり、ドミノの敷き詰めを描く `dominoboards` や形を描く `polyominoes` とは異なります。テストは画面から各パネルの分け方を読み戻し、左上のマスを含む領域の大きさ、両方の領域が連結していること、重複がないことを確認し、左上のマスから連結集合を育て残りも連結なものを残す列挙と集合が完全に一致することを確認し、インク・height・色調・occlusion を点ごとに比較します。現在のカタログは1160種で、plain を含む gallery は1161項目です。

- `chorddiagrams`：弦図。ある種類の弦図（円周上の点を直線の弦で2つずつ結んだもの）を1つずつ描きます。6 点と 8 点の全 15・105 個、8 点と 10 点の弦が交差しない 14・42 個（カタラン数）、8 点と 10 点の回転で同じものを1つとみなした 18・105 類です。点と弦の色調と height は交差の数で決まります。濃い炭黒の地に銀白色です。seed % 6 が種類を、⌊seed / 6⌋ がパネルの並び順と回転類の回転を選びます。完全な周期模様で、模式図であり、多角形を切り分ける `polygondissections` とは異なります。テストは画面から各パネルの弦図とレベルを読み戻し、各図がその種類であること、重複がないことを確認し、点の組をすべて選ぶ列挙と集合が完全に一致することを確認し、インク・height・色調・occlusion を点ごとに比較します。現在のカタログは1159種で、plain を含む gallery は1160項目です。

- `stilllifes`：ライフゲームの静物。コンウェイのライフゲームで、小さな箱の中のすべての still life（規則のもとで変化しないパターン）を1つずつ描きます。3×3・幅3高さ4・4×4・幅6高さ3の箱の全 11・27・82・91 個と、4×4 と幅6高さ3の箱で生きたセルがつながっている 74・69 個です。生きたセルは平らなタイルで、色調と height は生きた隣接セルの数（2 個は低く、3 個は高く）で決まります。濃い紺黒の地にスカイブルーです。seed % 6 が種類を、⌊seed / 6⌋ がパネルの並び順を選びます。完全な周期模様で、模式図であり、落ち着いていないランダムなスープの一場面を描く `gameoflife` とは異なります。テストは画面から各パネルのパターンを読み戻し、規則によって各パターンが静物であること、つながりの種類が本当につながっていること、重複がないことを確認し、箱の 2^(幅×高さ) 通りすべてを規則で試す列挙と集合が完全に一致することを確認し、インク・height・色調・occlusion を点ごとに比較します。現在のカタログは1158種で、plain を含む gallery は1159項目です。

- `alternatingsignmatrices`：交代符号行列。ある種類の交代符号行列（0・1・−1 の正方行列で、各行と各列の和が 1 で、0 でない要素の符号が交互になるもの）を1つずつ描きます。3・4・5 次の全 7・42・429 個、4 次で −1 を含む 18 個、5 次の対称なもの 67 個、5 次でちょうど 1 つの −1 を持つもの 200 個です。1 と −1 のマスだけが平らなタイルで（−1 は暗く低い）、濃いオリーブ黒の地にライムグリーンです。seed % 6 が種類を、⌊seed / 6⌋ がパネルの並び順を選びます。完全な周期模様で、模式図であり、4 次の 24 個は置換行列なので `permutations` と一部重なります。テストは画面から各パネルの行列を読み戻し、定義によって各行列が交代符号行列でその種類であること、重複がないことを確認し、単調三角形による列挙と集合が完全に一致することを確認し、インク・height・色調・occlusion を点ごとに比較します。現在のカタログは1157種で、plain を含む gallery は1158項目です。

- `polysticks`：ポリスティック。あるサイズのポリスティック（正方格子の単位辺がつながった形）を1つずつ描きます。free（回転と鏡像を同じとみなし、各パネルは8通りの回転・反転の1つで描く）の 3・4・5 本の 5・16・55 個と、fixed（平行移動だけで同じになるものを同じとみなす）の 3・4・5 本の 22・88・372 個です。棒は先が丸い線で、つながった棒に継ぎ目はなく、形の色調と height は対称性（8通りの回転・反転のうち自分自身に写るものの数 1・2・4・8）で決まります。濃い青緑黒の地に青緑色です。seed % 6 が種類を、⌊seed / 6⌋ がパネルの並び順と free の形の回転を選びます。完全な周期模様で、模式図であり、正方形のマスの `polyominoes` や格子点上の経路の `selfavoidingwalks` とは異なります。テストは画面から各パネルの形と対称性レベルを読み戻し、各形がちょうど n 本で連結し重複しないことを確認し、窓の中の n 本の辺の集合をすべて走査して連結なものを残す列挙と集合が完全に一致することを確認し、インク・height・色調・occlusion を点ごとに比較します。現在のカタログは1156種で、plain を含む gallery は1157項目です。
